   1: name: Build Android APK
   2: 
   3: on:
   4:   push:
   5:     branches: [ main ]
   6:   workflow_dispatch:
   7: 
   8: permissions:
   9:   contents: write
  10: 
  11: jobs:
  12:   build:
  13:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
  14:     steps:
  15:       - uses: actions/checkout@v4
  16:       - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
  17:         with:
  18:           distribution: 'temurin'
  19:           java-version: '21'
  20:       - uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
  21:         with:
  22:           channel: 'stable'
  23: 
  24:       - name: Criar estrutura Android, embutir TorrServer e configurar Auto-Update
  25:         run: |
  26:           flutter create . --platforms=android --org com.chiavegatti.cinema
  27: 
  28:           if [ ! -f cinema-release.jks ]; then
  29:             keytool -genkeypair -v -keystore cinema-release.jks -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 -alias cinema -storepass cinema35pass -keypass cinema35pass -dname "CN=Cinema, OU=App, O=Chiavegatti, L=Uberlandia, S=MG, C=BR"
  30:           fi
  31:           cp cinema-release.jks android/app/cinema-release.jks
  32: 
  33:           sed -i 's|<application|<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET"/><uses-permission android:name="android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO"/><uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE"/><uses-permission android:name="android.permission.REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES"/><application android:usesCleartextTraffic="true"|' android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
  34:           sed -i 's/android:label="app_cinema"/android:label="CineMax"/' android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
  35: 
  36:           # Adicionar FileProvider no AndroidManifest.xml para instalacao OTA do APK
  37:           sed -i 's|</application>|    <provider android:name="androidx.core.content.FileProvider" android:authorities="${applicationId}.fileprovider" android:exported="false" android:grantUriPermissions="true"><meta-data android:name="android.support.FILE_PROVIDER_PATHS" android:resource="@xml/file_paths" /></provider>\n    </application>|' android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
  38: 
  39:           mkdir -p android/app/src/main/res/xml
  40:           cat << 'XMLEOF' > android/app/src/main/res/xml/file_paths.xml
  41:           <?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
  42:           <paths>
  43:               <files-path name="updates" path="." />
  44:               <cache-path name="cache_updates" path="." />
  45:           </paths>
  46:           XMLEOF
  47: 
  48:           # Configurar assinatura fixa release no Gradle
  49:           cat << 'PROPEOF' > android/key.properties
  50:           storePassword=cinema35pass
  51:           keyPassword=cinema35pass
  52:           keyAlias=cinema
  53:           storeFile=cinema-release.jks
  54:           PROPEOF
  55: 
  56:           if [ -f android/app/build.gradle.kts ]; then
  57:             cat << 'GRADLEKTS' >> android/app/build.gradle.kts
  58: 
  59:           android {
  60:               packaging {
  61:                   jniLibs {
  62:                       useLegacyPackaging = true
  63:                   }
  64:               }
  65:               signingConfigs {
  66:                   create("releaseFixed") {
  67:                       storeFile = file("cinema-release.jks")
  68:                       storePassword = "cinema35pass"
  69:                       keyAlias = "cinema"
  70:                       keyPassword = "cinema35pass"
  71:                   }
  72:               }
  73:               buildTypes {
  74:                   getByName("release") {
  75:                       signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("releaseFixed")
  76:                   }
  77:               }
  78:           }
  79:           GRADLEKTS
  80:           elif [ -f android/app/build.gradle ]; then
  81:             cat << 'GRADLEGROOVY' >> android/app/build.gradle
  82: 
  83:           android {
  84:               packagingOptions {
  85:                   jniLibs {
  86:                       useLegacyPackaging = true
  87:                   }
  88:               }
  89:               signingConfigs {
  90:                   releaseFixed {
  91:                       storeFile file("cinema-release.jks")
  92:                       storePassword "cinema35pass"
  93:                       keyAlias "cinema"
  94:                       keyPassword "cinema35pass"
  95:                   }
  96:               }
  97:               buildTypes {
  98:                   release {
  99:                       signingConfig signingConfigs.releaseFixed
 100:                   }
 101:               }
 102:           }
 103:           GRADLEGROOVY
 104:           fi
 105: 
 106:           mkdir -p android/app/src/main/jniLibs/arm64-v8a
 107:           curl -fsSL -o android/app/src/main/jniLibs/arm64-v8a/libtorrserver.so https://github.com/YouROK/TorrServer/releases/latest/download/TorrServer-android-arm64
 108:           chmod +x android/app/src/main/jniLibs/arm64-v8a/libtorrserver.so
 109: 
 110:           MAIN_ACT=$(find android/app/src/main -name "MainActivity.kt" | head -n 1)
 111:           cat << 'KTEOF' > "$MAIN_ACT"
 112:           package com.chiavegatti.cinema.app_cinema
 113: 
 114:           import android.content.Intent
 115:           import android.net.Uri
 116:           import android.os.Build
 117:           import android.provider.Settings
 118:           import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
 119:           import io.flutter.embedding.android.FlutterActivity
 120:           import io.flutter.embedding.engine.FlutterEngine
 121:           import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
 122:           import java.io.File
 123: 
 124:           class MainActivity: FlutterActivity() {
 125:               private val CHANNEL = "com.chiavegatti.cinema/torrserver"
 126: 
 127:               override fun configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine: FlutterEngine) {
 128:                   super.configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine)
 129:                   MethodChannel(flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger, CHANNEL).setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
 130:                       when (call.method) {
 131:                           "getPaths" -> {
 132:                               val paths = mapOf(
 133:                                   "nativeLibDir" to applicationContext.applicationInfo.nativeLibraryDir,
 134:                                   "filesDir" to applicationContext.filesDir.absolutePath
 135:                               )
 136:                               result.success(paths)
 137:                           }
 138:                           "installApk" -> {
 139:                               val path = call.argument<String>("path")
 140:                               if (path.isNullOrEmpty()) {
 141:                                   result.error("INVALID_PATH", "Caminho vazio", null)
 142:                                   return@setMethodCallHandler
 143:                               }
 144:                               try {
 145:                                   if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O && !packageManager.canRequestPackageInstalls()) {
 146:                                       val permIntent = Intent(
 147:                                           Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES,
 148:                                           Uri.parse("package:$packageName")
 149:                                       )
 150:                                       permIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
 151:                                       startActivity(permIntent)
 152:                                       result.success("PERMISSION_NEEDED")
 153:                                       return@setMethodCallHandler
 154:                                   }
 155:                                   val file = File(path)
 156:                                   val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
 157:                                       this,
 158:                                       "$packageName.fileprovider",
 159:                                       file
 160:                                   )
 161:                                   val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
 162:                                       setDataAndType(uri, "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
 163:                                       addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
 164:                                       addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
 165:                                   }
 166:                                   startActivity(intent)
 167:                                   result.success("STARTED")
 168:                               } catch (e: Exception) {
 169:                                   result.error("INSTALL_ERROR", e.message, null)
 170:                               }
 171:                           }
 172:                           else -> result.notImplemented()
 173:                       }
 174:                   }
 175:               }
 176:           }
 177:           KTEOF
 178: 
 179:           python3 -m pip install pillow
 180:           python3 gerar_icone.py
 181: 
 182:       - name: Instalar dependencias
 183:         run: flutter pub get
 184: 
 185:       - name: Compilar APK Release (arm64)
 186:         run: |
 187:           flutter build apk --config-only --release --target-platform android-arm64
 188:           cd android && ./gradlew assembleRelease -x lintVitalAnalyzeRelease -x lintVitalRelease
 189: 
 190:       - name: Publicar APK direto na Release
 191:         uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
 192:         with:
 193:           tag_name: latest-apk
 194:           name: CineMax APK (TorrServer Integrado)
 195:           files: build/app/outputs/flutter-apk/app-release.apk
 196: 
 197:       - name: Upload Artifact
 198:         uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
 199:         with:
 200:           name: app-cinema-release
 201:           path: build/app/outputs/flutter-apk/app-release.apk

  62: class CinemaBootstrapScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  63:   const CinemaBootstrapScreen({super.key});
  64: 
  65:   @override
  66:   State<CinemaBootstrapScreen> createState() => _CinemaBootstrapScreenState();
  67: }
  68: 
  69: class _CinemaBootstrapScreenState extends State<CinemaBootstrapScreen> {
  70:   List<ObraAgrupada> _catalogo = [];
  71:   bool _carregando = true;
  72:   bool? _modoTvForcado;
  73: 
  74:   @override
  75:   void initState() {
  76:     super.initState();
  77:     _inicializar();
  78:   }
  79: 
  80:   Future<void> _inicializar() async {
  81:     final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  82:     final modoSalvo = prefs.getBool('modo_tv_forcado');
  83:     final obras = await SupabaseService.carregarCatalogoAgrupado();
  84:     if (mounted) {
  85:       setState(() {
  86:         _modoTvForcado = modoSalvo;
  87:         _catalogo = obras;
  88:         _carregando = false;
  89:       });
  90:     }
  91:   }
  92: 
  93:   Future<void> _recarregarCatalogo() async {
  94:     setState(() => _carregando = true);
  95:     final obras = await SupabaseService.carregarCatalogoAgrupado(forcarRefresh: true);
  96:     if (mounted) {
  97:       setState(() {
  98:         _catalogo = obras;
  99:         _carregando = false;
 100:       });
 101:     }
 102:   }
 103: 
 104:   Future<void> _alternarModo(bool paraTv) async {
 105:     final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
 106:     await prefs.setBool('modo_tv_forcado', paraTv);
 107:     setState(() => _modoTvForcado = paraTv);
 108:   }
 109: 
 110:   void _abrirObra(ObraAgrupada obra) {
 111:     Navigator.push(
 112:       context,
 113:       MaterialPageRoute(
 114:         builder: (_) => DetalheObraScreen(
 115:           obra: obra,
 116:           catalogoCompleto: _catalogo,
 117:         ),
 118:       ),
 119:     );
 120:   }
 121: 
 122:   @override
 123:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 124:     if (_carregando) {
 125:       return const Scaffold(
 126:         backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0A0C10),
 127:         body: Center(
 128:           child: Column(
 129:             mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 130:             children: [
 131:               CinemaLogoWidget(height: 64, width: 220),
 132:               SizedBox(height: 24),
 133:               CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 134:             ],
 135:           ),
 136:         ),
 137:       );
 138:     }
 139: 
 140:     final size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;
 141:     final isTvAuto = size.shortestSide >= 650;
 142:     final usarLayoutTv = _modoTvForcado ?? isTvAuto;
 143: 
 144:     if (usarLayoutTv) {
 145:       SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations([
 146:         DeviceOrientation.landscapeLeft,
 147:         DeviceOrientation.landscapeRight,
 148:       ]);
 149:       return TvHomeScreen(
 150:         catalogo: _catalogo,
 151:         onRefresh: _recarregarCatalogo,
 152:         onAbrirObra: _abrirObra,
 153:         onAlternarModoMobile: () => _alternarModo(false),
 154:       );
 155:     }
 156: 
 157:     if (!CinemaOrientation.emPlayer) {
 158:       SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations([
 159:         DeviceOrientation.portraitUp,
 160:       ]);
 161:     }
 162:     return MobileHomeScreen(
 163:       catalogo: _catalogo,
 164:       onRefresh: _recarregarCatalogo,
 165:       onAbrirObra: _abrirObra,
 166:       onAlternarModoTv: () => _alternarModo(true),
 167:     );
 168:   }
 169: }
 170: 
 171: class MobileHomeScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 172:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 173:   final Future<void> Function() onRefresh;
 174:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 175:   final VoidCallback onAlternarModoTv;
 176: 
 177:   const MobileHomeScreen({
 178:     super.key,
 179:     required this.catalogo,
 180:     required this.onRefresh,
 181:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 182:     required this.onAlternarModoTv,
 183:   });
 184: 
 185:   @override
 186:   State<MobileHomeScreen> createState() => _MobileHomeScreenState();
 187: }
 188: 
 189: class _MobileHomeScreenState extends State<MobileHomeScreen> {
 190:   int _abaIndex = 0;
 191:   static const List<String> _abas = ['Início', 'Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'];
 192: 
 193:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _listaAba {
 194:     if (_abaIndex == 0) return widget.catalogo;
 195:     if (_abaIndex == 1) {
 196:       return widget.catalogo.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('filme') && !o.isSerie).toList();
 197:     }
 198:     if (_abaIndex == 2) {
 199:       return widget.catalogo.where((o) => o.isSerie).toList();
 200:     }
 201:     if (_abaIndex == 3) {
 202:       return widget.catalogo.where((o) {
 203:         final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
 204:         final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 205:         return c.contains('kids') || c.contains('infantil') || g.contains('animacao');
 206:       }).toList();
 207:     }
 208:     return widget.catalogo.where((o) {
 209:       final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
 210:       final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 211:       return c.contains('anime') || g.contains('anime');
 212:     }).toList();
 213:   }
 214: 
 215:   List<ObraAgrupada> _porGenero(List<ObraAgrupada> base, String termo) {
 216:     final q = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(termo);
 217:     return base.where((o) {
 218:       final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 219:       final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
 220:       return g.contains(q) || t.any((x) => x.contains(q));
 221:     }).toList();
 222:   }
 223: 
 224:   @override
 225:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 226:     final base = _listaAba;
 227:     final destaques = base.where((o) => o.emDestaque).take(6).toList();
 228:     if (destaques.isEmpty && base.isNotEmpty) {
 229:       destaques.addAll(base.take(5));
 230:     }
 231: 
 232:     return Scaffold(
 233:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0A0C10),
 234:       appBar: AppBar(
 235:         title: const CinemaLogoWidget(height: 34, width: 120),
 236:         actions: [
 237:           IconButton(
 238:             tooltip: 'Buscar',
 239:             icon: const Icon(Icons.search),
 240:             onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
 241:               context,
 242:               MaterialPageRoute(
 243:                 builder: (_) => TvBuscaScreen(catalogo: widget.catalogo, onAbrirObra: widget.onAbrirObra),
 244:               ),
 245:             ),
 246:           ),
 247:           IconButton(
 248:             tooltip: 'Filtro Avançado',
 249:             icon: const Icon(Icons.filter_alt_outlined),
 250:             onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
 251:               context,
 252:               MaterialPageRoute(
 253:                 builder: (_) => TvFiltroScreen(catalogo: widget.catalogo, onAbrirObra: widget.onAbrirObra),
 254:               ),
 255:             ),
 256:           ),
 257:           IconButton(
 258:             tooltip: 'Meus Favoritos e Histórico',
 259:             icon: const Icon(Icons.history),
 260:             onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
 261:               context,
 262:               MaterialPageRoute(
 263:                 builder: (_) => TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen(catalogo: widget.catalogo, onAbrirObra: widget.onAbrirObra),
 264:               ),
 265:             ),
 266:           ),
 267:           IconButton(
 268:             tooltip: 'Modo TV Box',
 269:             icon: const Icon(Icons.tv, color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 270:             onPressed: widget.onAlternarModoTv,
 271:           ),
 272:         ],
 273:       ),
 274:       body: Column(
 275:         children: [
 276:           SizedBox(
 277:             height: 44,
 278:             child: ListView.separated(

 280:               scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
 281:               itemCount: _abas.length,
 282:               separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 8),
 283:               itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
 284:                 final ativo = idx == _abaIndex;
 285:                 return ChoiceChip(
 286:                   label: Text(_abas[idx]),
 287:                   selected: ativo,
 288:                   selectedColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 289:                   labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ativo ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 290:                   onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _abaIndex = idx),
 291:                 );
 292:               },
 293:             ),
 294:           ),
 295:           const SizedBox(height: 8),
 296:           Expanded(
 297:             child: RefreshIndicator(
 298:               color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 299:               onRefresh: widget.onRefresh,
 300:               child: ListView(
 301:                 padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 6, 14, 24),
 302:                 children: [
 303:                   if (destaques.isNotEmpty)
 304:                     SizedBox(
 305:                       height: 190,
 306:                       child: PageView.builder(
 307:                         itemCount: destaques.length,
 308:                         itemBuilder: (_, idx) {

 310:                           final img = o.bannerUrl.isNotEmpty ? o.bannerUrl : o.capaUrl;
 311:                           return GestureDetector(
 312:                             onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(o),
 313:                             child: ClipRRect(

 315:                               child: Stack(

 317:                                 children: [

 319:                                     CachedNetworkImage(imageUrl: img, fit: BoxFit.cover)
 320:                                   else

   1: class FonteMidia {
   2:   final String id;
   3:   final String titulo;
   4:   final String categoria;
   5:   final String urlStream;
   6:   final String urlStreamBackup;
   7:   final String formato;
   8:   final String resolucao;
   9:   final String tipoAudio;
  10:   final List<String> idiomasAudio;
  11:   final Map<String, String> headers;
  12:   final int? temporada;
  13:   final int? episodio;
  14: 
  15:   FonteMidia({
  16:     required this.id,
  17:     required this.titulo,
  18:     required this.categoria,
  19:     required this.urlStream,
  20:     required this.urlStreamBackup,
  21:     required this.formato,
  22:     required this.resolucao,
  23:     required this.tipoAudio,
  24:     required this.idiomasAudio,
  25:     required this.headers,
  26:     this.temporada,
  27:     this.episodio,
  28:   });
  29: 
  30:   String get badgeResumo =>
  31:       '${resolucao.isNotEmpty ? resolucao : 'HD'} • ${tipoAudio.isNotEmpty ? tipoAudio : 'Dublado'}';
  32: 
  33:   String get etiquetaEpisodio =>
  34:       (temporada != null && episodio != null) ? 'S$temporada - E$episodio' : '';
  35: 
  36:   factory FonteMidia.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
  37:     final rawHeaders = json['headers_customizados'];
  38:     final Map<String, String> parsedHeaders = {};
  39:     if (rawHeaders is Map) {
  40:       rawHeaders.forEach((k, v) {
  41:         if (k != null && v != null) parsedHeaders[k.toString()] = v.toString();
  42:       });
  43:     }
  44:     final rawIdiomas = json['idiomas_audio'];
  45:     return FonteMidia(
  46:       id: (json['id'] ?? '').toString(),
  47:       titulo: (json['titulo'] ?? 'Sem Título').toString(),
  48:       categoria: (json['categoria'] ?? 'Filme').toString(),
  49:       urlStream: (json['url_stream'] ?? '').toString(),
  50:       urlStreamBackup: (json['url_stream_backup'] ?? '').toString(),
  51:       formato: (json['formato'] ?? 'mp4').toString(),
  52:       resolucao: (json['resolucao'] ?? '1080p').toString(),
  53:       tipoAudio: (json['tipo_audio'] ?? 'Dublado').toString(),
  54:       idiomasAudio: rawIdiomas is List
  55:           ? rawIdiomas.map((e) => e.toString()).toList()
  56:           : ['Português'],
  57:       headers: parsedHeaders,
  58:       temporada: json['temporada'] is int
  59:           ? json['temporada']
  60:           : int.tryParse('${json['temporada'] ?? ''}'),
  61:       episodio: json['episodio'] is int
  62:           ? json['episodio']
  63:           : int.tryParse('${json['episodio'] ?? ''}'),
  64:     );
  65:   }
  66: }
  67: 
  68: class ObraAgrupada {
  69:   final String chaveGrupo;
  70:   final int? tmdbId;
  71:   final String titulo;
  72:   final String tituloOriginal;
  73:   final String categoria;
  74:   final String genero;
  75:   final String sinopse;
  76:   final String capaUrl;
  77:   final String bannerUrl;
  78:   final String ano;
  79:   final String pais;
  80:   final String diretor;
  81:   final String elenco;
  82:   final int aberturaSegundos;
  83:   final bool emDestaque;
  84:   final int ordemDestaque;
  85:   final List<String> tags;
  86:   final double notaImdb;
  87:   final int visualizacoes;
  88:   final List<FonteMidia> fontes;
  89: 
  90:   ObraAgrupada({
  91:     required this.chaveGrupo,
  92:     this.tmdbId,
  93:     required this.titulo,
  94:     this.tituloOriginal = '',
  95:     required this.categoria,
  96:     required this.genero,
  97:     required this.sinopse,
  98:     required this.capaUrl,
  99:     required this.bannerUrl,
 100:     required this.ano,
 101:     this.pais = '',
 102:     this.diretor = '',
 103:     this.elenco = '',
 104:     this.aberturaSegundos = 85,
 105:     required this.emDestaque,
 106:     required this.ordemDestaque,
 107:     this.tags = const [],
 108:     this.notaImdb = 0.0,
 109:     this.visualizacoes = 0,
 110:     required this.fontes,
 111:   });
 112: 
 113:   bool get disponivelParaAssistir => fontes.isNotEmpty;
 114: 
 115:   bool get isSerie =>
 116:       categoria.toLowerCase().contains('série') ||
 117:       categoria.toLowerCase().contains('serie') ||
 118:       fontes.any((f) => f.episodio != null);
 119: 
 120:   double get scorePopularidade => (visualizacoes * 2.0) + (notaImdb * 10.0);
 121: 
 122:   String get badgePoster {
 123:     if (!isSerie) return '';
 124:     final temps = temporadasDisponiveis;
 125:     if (temps.length > 1) return 'Temp.${temps.last}';
 126:     final eps = episodiosDaTemporada(temps.first);
 127:     if (eps.isNotEmpty && eps.last.episodio != null) {
 128:       return 'S${temps.first}-E${eps.last.episodio}';
 129:     }
 130:     return 'Temp.${temps.first}';
 131:   }
 132: 
 133:   List<String> get audiosDisponiveis {
 134:     final set = <String>{};
 135:     for (final f in fontes) {
 136:       if (f.tipoAudio.isNotEmpty) set.add(f.tipoAudio);
 137:       for (final idm in f.idiomasAudio) {
 138:         if (idm.trim().isNotEmpty) set.add(idm.trim());
 139:       }
 140:     }
 141:     return set.isEmpty ? ['Português'] : set.toList();
 142:   }
 143: 
 144:   List<String> get resolucoesDisponiveis {
 145:     final set = <String>{};
 146:     for (final f in fontes) {
 147:       if (f.resolucao.isNotEmpty) set.add(f.resolucao);
 148:     }
 149:     return set.isEmpty ? ['1080p'] : set.toList();
 150:   }
 151: 
 152:   List<int> get temporadasDisponiveis {
 153:     final set = <int>{};
 154:     for (final f in fontes) {
 155:       set.add(f.temporada ?? 1);
 156:     }
 157:     if (set.isEmpty) return [1];
 158:     return set.toList()..sort();
 159:   }
 160: 
 161:   List<FonteMidia> episodiosDaTemporada(int temp) {
 162:     final list = fontes.where((f) => (f.temporada ?? 1) == temp).toList();
 163:     list.sort((a, b) => (a.episodio ?? 0).compareTo(b.episodio ?? 0));
 164:     return list;
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   ObraAgrupada copyWith({
 168:     String? tituloOriginal,
 169:     String? pais,
 170:     String? diretor,
 171:     String? elenco,
 172:     String? sinopse,
 173:     String? genero,
 174:     double? notaImdb,
 175:   }) {
 176:     return ObraAgrupada(
 177:       chaveGrupo: chaveGrupo,
 178:       tmdbId: tmdbId,
 179:       titulo: titulo,
 180:       tituloOriginal: tituloOriginal ?? this.tituloOriginal,
 181:       categoria: categoria,
 182:       genero: (genero != null && genero.isNotEmpty) ? genero : this.genero,
 183:       sinopse: (sinopse != null && sinopse.isNotEmpty) ? sinopse : this.sinopse,
 184:       capaUrl: capaUrl,
 185:       bannerUrl: bannerUrl,
 186:       ano: ano,
 187:       pais: (pais != null && pais.isNotEmpty) ? pais : this.pais,
 188:       diretor: (diretor != null && diretor.isNotEmpty) ? diretor : this.diretor,
 189:       elenco: (elenco != null && elenco.isNotEmpty) ? elenco : this.elenco,
 190:       aberturaSegundos: aberturaSegundos,
 191:       emDestaque: emDestaque,
 192:       ordemDestaque: ordemDestaque,
 193:       tags: tags,
 194:       notaImdb: (notaImdb != null && notaImdb > 0) ? notaImdb : this.notaImdb,
 195:       visualizacoes: visualizacoes,
 196:       fontes: fontes,
 197:     );
 198:   }
 199: }

   6: class AbaCategoriaPage extends StatefulWidget {

  10:   const AbaCategoriaPage({super.key, required this.nomeAba, required this.obras, required this.onAbrirObra});

  15: class _AbaCategoriaPageState extends State<AbaCategoriaPage> {
  16:   final PageController _bannerCtrl = PageController();
  17:   Timer? _timer;
  18:   int _bannerIdx = 0;
  19:   Map<String, Map<String, dynamic>> _progresso = {};
  20: 
  21:   @override
  22:   void initState() {
  23:     super.initState();
  24:     _carregarProgresso();
  25:     _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 5), (_) {
  26:       if (_bannerCtrl.hasClients) {
  27:         final next = (_bannerIdx + 1) % 4;
  28:         _bannerCtrl.animateToPage(next, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 450), curve: Curves.easeInOut);
  29:       }
  30:     });
  31:   }
  32: 
  33:   Future<void> _carregarProgresso() async {
  34:     final p = await SupabaseService.carregarProgressoAssistido();
  35:     if (mounted) setState(() => _progresso = p);
  36:   }
  37: 
  38:   @override
  39:   void dispose() {
  40:     _timer?.cancel();
  41:     _bannerCtrl.dispose();
  42:     super.dispose();
  43:   }
  44: 
  45:   bool _temTag(ObraAgrupada o, List<String> chaves) {
  46:     final tgs = o.tags.map((e) => e.toLowerCase()).toList();
  47:     return chaves.any((c) => tgs.any((t) => t.contains(c.toLowerCase())));
  48:   }
  49: 
  50:   bool _temGenero(ObraAgrupada o, String g) {
  51:     return o.genero.toLowerCase().contains(g.toLowerCase()) || _temTag(o, [g]);
  52:   }
  53: 
  54:   List<ObraAgrupada> _filtrarPorAba() {
  55:     final cat = widget.nomeAba.toLowerCase();
  56:     return widget.obras.where((o) {
  57:       final c = o.categoria.toLowerCase();
  58:       final g = o.genero.toLowerCase();
  59:       if (cat.contains('recomenda')) return true;
  60:       if (cat.contains('filme')) return c.contains('filme') && !c.contains('tv');
  61:       if (cat.contains('série') || cat.contains('serie')) return c.contains('série') || c.contains('serie');
  62:       if (cat.contains('infantil')) return c.contains('infantil') || g.contains('infantil') || g.contains('anima');
  63:       if (cat.contains('anime')) return c.contains('anime') || g.contains('anime');
  64:       return true;
  65:     }).toList();
  66:   }
  67: 
  68:   void _abrir(ObraAgrupada o) {
  69:     widget.onAbrirObra(o);
  70:   }
  71: 
  72:   void _verTodos(String titulo, List<ObraAgrupada> lista) {
  73:     Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => Scaffold(
  74:       backgroundColor: Colors.black,
  75:       appBar: AppBar(backgroundColor: Colors.black, title: Text(titulo, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white))),
  76:       body: GridView.builder(
  77:         padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
  78:         gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount: 3, childAspectRatio: 0.62, crossAxisSpacing: 10, mainAxisSpacing: 12),
  79:         itemCount: lista.length,
  80:         itemBuilder: (_, i) {
  81:           final o = lista[i];
  82:           return GestureDetector(
  83:             onTap: () { Navigator.pop(context); _abrir(o); },
  84:             child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
  85:               Expanded(child: ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), child: o.capaUrl.isNotEmpty ? Image.network(o.capaUrl, fit: BoxFit.cover, width: double.infinity) : Container(color: Colors.grey[900]))),
  86:               const SizedBox(height: 4),
  87:               Text(o.titulo, maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11)),
  88:             ]),
  89:           );
  90:         },
  91:       ),
  92:     )));
  93:   }
  94: 
  95:   String _etiquetaSerie(ObraAgrupada o) {
  96:     final c = o.categoria.toLowerCase();
  97:     if (!c.contains('série') && !c.contains('serie') && !c.contains('anime') && o.fontes.length <= 1) return '';
  98:     int maxT = 0, maxE = 0;
  99:     for (final f in o.fontes) {
 100:       final t = f.temporada ?? 0;
 101:       final e = f.episodio ?? 0;
 102:       if (t > maxT) { maxT = t; maxE = e; }
 103:       else if (t == maxT && e > maxE) { maxE = e; }
 104:     }
 105:     if (maxT > 0 && maxE > 0) return 'S$maxT - E$maxE';
 106:     if (maxT > 0) return 'Temp.$maxT';
 107:     return '';
 108:   }
 109: 
 110:   @override
 111:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 112:     final lista = widget.obras;
 113:     if (lista.isEmpty) {
 114:       return Center(child: Text('Nenhum item em ${widget.nomeAba}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)));
 115:     }
 116:     final cat = widget.nomeAba.toLowerCase();
 117:     final isFilmes = cat.contains('filme');
 118:     final isSeries = cat.contains('série') || cat.contains('serie');
 119:     final isInfantil = cat.contains('infantil');
 120:     final isAnime = cat.contains('anime');
 121:     final isRecomenda = cat.contains('recomenda');
 122: 
 123:     final banners = lista.where((o) => o.emDestaque || _temTag(o, ['banner', 'destaque'])).toList();
 124:     if (banners.length < 4) {
 125:       for (final o in lista) {
 126:         if (!banners.contains(o) && (o.bannerUrl.isNotEmpty || o.capaUrl.isNotEmpty)) banners.add(o);
 127:         if (banners.length == 4) break;
 128:       }
 129:     }
 130:     final top4 = banners.take(4).toList();
 131: 
 132:     return ListView(
 133:         padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
 134:         children: [
 135:           if (top4.isNotEmpty) _buildBanner(top4),
 136:           if (isRecomenda) ..._buildSecoesRecomendacoes(lista),
 137:           if (isFilmes) ..._buildSecoesFilmes(lista),
 138:           if (isSeries) ..._buildSecoesSeries(lista),
 139:           if (!isRecomenda && !isFilmes && !isSeries) _buildFileira('Catálogo ${widget.nomeAba}', lista),
 140:         ],
 141:     );
 142:   }
 143: 
 144:   List<Widget> _buildSecoesRecomendacoes(List<ObraAgrupada> lista) {
 145:     final live = widget.obras.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('tv') || _temTag(o, ['live', 'tv'])).toList();
 146:     final cont = widget.obras.where((o) => _progresso.containsKey(o.chaveGrupo)).toList();
 147:     final topFilmes = [...lista.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('filme'))]..sort((a, b) => b.visualizacoes.compareTo(a.visualizacoes));
 148:     final topSeries = [...lista.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('série') || o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('serie'))]..sort((a, b) => b.visualizacoes.compareTo(a.visualizacoes));
 149:     return [
 150:       if (live.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Livestream', live, horizontal: true, iconeLive: true),
 151:       if (cont.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Continuar Assistindo', cont, mostrarProgresso: true),
 152:       if (topFilmes.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Top Filmes', topFilmes, ranking: true),
 153:       if (topSeries.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Top Séries', topSeries, ranking: true),
 154:       _buildFileira('Adicionados Recentemente', lista),
 155:     ];
 156:   }
 157: 
 158:   List<Widget> _buildSecoesFilmes(List<ObraAgrupada> filmes) {
 159:     final noCinema = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['no cinema', 'cinema'])).toList();
 160:     final lancamentos = [...filmes]..sort((a, b) => b.ano.compareTo(a.ano));
 161:     final dublados = filmes.where((o) => o.fontes.any((f) => f.tipoAudio.toLowerCase().contains('dub') || f.tipoAudio.toLowerCase().contains('dual'))).toList();
 162:     final netflix = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['netflix'])).toList();
 163:     final especiais = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['especiais', 'especial'])).toList();
 164:     final disney = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['disney'])).toList();
 165:     final nacional = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['nacional']) || _temGenero(o, 'nacional')).toList();
 166:     final curta = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['curta']) || _temGenero(o, 'curta')).toList();
 167:     final emBreve = filmes.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['em breve', 'trailer']) || o.titulo.toLowerCase().contains('(trailer)')).toList();
 168: 
 169:     final generos = [
 170:       'Ação', 'Aventura', 'Romance', 'Mistério', 'Crime', 'Comédia', 'Drama',
 171:       'Família', 'Fantasia', 'Ficção Científica', 'Terror', 'Suspense', 'Musical',
 172:       'Guerra', 'Faroeste', 'Documentário', 'História'
 173:     ];
 174: 
 175:     return [
 176:       if (noCinema.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('No Cinema', noCinema),
 177:       if (lancamentos.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Lançamentos', lancamentos),
 178:       if (dublados.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Dublado', dublados),
 179:       if (netflix.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Netflix Filmes', netflix),
 180:       if (especiais.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Especiais', especiais, horizontal: true),
 181:       if (disney.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Disney+ Filmes', disney),
 182:       if (nacional.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Nacional', nacional),
 183:       for (final g in generos)
 184:         if (filmes.any((o) => _temGenero(o, g)))
 185:           _buildFileira(g, filmes.where((o) => _temGenero(o, g)).toList()),
 186:       if (curta.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Curta', curta, horizontal: true),
 187:       if (emBreve.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Em breve', emBreve),
 188:       _buildFileira('Todos', filmes),
 189:     ];
 190:   }
 191: 
 192:   List<Widget> _buildSecoesSeries(List<ObraAgrupada> series) {
 193:     final melhores = [...series]..sort((a, b) => b.notaImdb.compareTo(a.notaImdb));
 194:     final doramas = series.where((o) => _temGenero(o, 'dorama') || _temTag(o, ['dorama', 'corean'])).toList();
 195:     final turcas = series.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['turca', 'novelas turcas'])).toList();
 196:     final novelas = series.where((o) => _temGenero(o, 'novela') || _temTag(o, ['novela'])).toList();
 197:     final plataformas = [
 198:       ['Netflix', 'netflix'],
 199:       ['Disney+', 'disney'],
 200:       ['MAX', 'max'],
 201:       ['Apple TV+', 'apple'],
 202:       ['Paramount +', 'paramount'],
 203:       ['Prime Video', 'prime'],
 204:       ['Globo', 'globo'],
 205:       ['Hulu', 'hulu'],
 206:     ];
 207:     final nacionais = series.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['nacional', 'nacionais'])).toList();
 208:     final renovadas = series.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['renovada'])).toList();
 209:     final biblicas = series.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['bíblica', 'biblica'])).toList();
 210:     final latinas = series.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['latino'])).toList();
 211:     final britanicas = series.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['britânica', 'britanica'])).toList();
 212:     final generos = [
 213:       'Drama Médico', 'Drama', 'Família', 'Fantasia', 'Procedimento Policial',
 214:       'Aventura', 'Comédia', 'Ação', 'Suspense', 'Terror', 'Romance', 'Infantil', 'Esporte'
 215:     ];
 216: 
 217:     return [
 218:       _buildFileira('Última Temporada e Episódio', series),
 219:       if (melhores.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Melhores Séries', melhores),
 220:       if (doramas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Doramas', doramas),
 221:       if (turcas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Novelas Turcas', turcas),
 222:       if (novelas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Novelas', novelas),
 223:       for (final p in plataformas)
 224:         if (series.any((o) => _temTag(o, [p[1]])))
 225:           _buildFileira(p[0], series.where((o) => _temTag(o, [p[1]])).toList()),
 226:       if (nacionais.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Séries Nacionais', nacionais),
 227:       if (renovadas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Séries Renovadas', renovadas),
 228:       if (biblicas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Telenovelas Bíblicas', biblicas),
 229:       for (final g in generos)
 230:         if (series.any((o) => _temGenero(o, g)))
 231:           _buildFileira(g, series.where((o) => _temGenero(o, g)).toList()),
 232:       if (latinas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Séries Latino-americanas', latinas),
 233:       if (britanicas.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Séries Britânicas', britanicas),
 234:       _buildFileira('Todas', series),
 235:     ];
 236:   }
 237: 
 238: 
 239:   bool _combina(ObraAgrupada o, List<String> termos) {
 240:     final tit = o.titulo.toLowerCase();
 241:     return _temTag(o, termos) || termos.any((k) => tit.contains(k.toLowerCase()));
 242:   }
 243: 
 244:   List<Widget> _buildSecoesInfantil(List<ObraAgrupada> inf) {
 245:     final guiaPais = inf.where((o) => _temGenero(o, 'família') || _temTag(o, ['guia dos pais'])).toList();
 246:     final cantar = inf.where((o) => _temGenero(o, 'musical') || _temTag(o, ['cantar', 'dançar', 'musical'])).toList();
 247:     final seriesDesenho = inf.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('série') || o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('serie') || o.fontes.length > 1).toList();
 248:     final liveAction = inf.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['live-action', 'live action', 'remake'])).toList();
 249:     final animal = inf.where((o) => _temTag(o, ['animal', 'animais'])).toList();
 250:     final filmesAnim = inf.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('filme') && o.fontes.length == 1).toList();
 251: 
 252:     final franquias = [
 253:       ['Pixar', ['pixar']],
 254:       ['Kung Fu Panda', ['kung fu panda']],
 255:       ['Monstros', ['monstros', 'monsters']],
 256:       ['SpongeBob', ['spongebob', 'bob esponja', 'kamp coral']],
 257:       ['Disney 100', ['disney 100', 'disney']],
 258:       ['Garfield', ['garfield']],
 259:       ['Patrulha Canina', ['patrulha canina', 'paw patrol']],
 260:       ['Minions', ['minions', 'meu malvado favorito']],
 261:       ['Snoopy', ['snoopy', 'peanuts']],
 262:       ['Mickey', ['mickey']],
 263:       ['Madagascar', ['madagascar', 'pinguins']],
 264:       ['DreamWorks', ['dreamworks']],
 265:       ['Elsa', ['frozen', 'elsa']],
 266:       ['Bebê', ['bebê', 'bebe', 'baby']],
 267:       ['Barbie', ['barbie']],
 268:       ['Pony', ['pony', 'my little pony']],
 269:       ['Peppa Pig', ['peppa']],
 270:     ];
 271: 
 272:     return [
 273:       _buildFileira('Todos', inf),
 274:       if (guiaPais.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Com a Família (Guia dos Pais)', guiaPais),
 275:       if (cantar.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Vamos Cantar e Dançar \ud83c\udfb6', cantar),
 276:       if (seriesDesenho.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Séries de Desenhos', seriesDesenho),
 277:       if (liveAction.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Live-action e Remakes', liveAction),
 278:       if (animal.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Animal Engraçado', animal),
 279:       if (guiaPais.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Diversão em Família', guiaPais),
 280:       if (filmesAnim.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Filmes de Animação', filmesAnim),
 281:       for (final f in franquias)
 282:         if (inf.any((o) => _combina(o, f[1] as List<String>)))
 283:           _buildFileira(f[0] as String, inf.where((o) => _combina(o, f[1] as List<String>)).toList()),
 284:     ];
 285:   }

 287:   List<Widget> _buildSecoesAnime(List<ObraAgrupada> animes) {
 288:     final filmesAnime = animes.where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('filme') || (o.fontes.length == 1 && (o.fontes.first.temporada ?? 0) == 0)).toList();
 289: 
 290:     final franquias = [
 291:       ['One piece', ['one piece']],
 292:       ['Naruto', ['naruto', 'boruto']],
 293:       ['Dragon Ball', ['dragon ball']],
 294:       ['Ataque dos Titãs', ['ataque dos titãs', 'attack on titan']],
 295:       ['My Hero Academia', ['my hero academia', 'boku no hero']],
 296:       ['Pokémon', ['pokémon', 'pokemon', 'digimon']],
 297:       ['JoJo’s Bizarre Adventure', ['jojo']],
 298:       ['Bleach', ['bleach']],
 299:       ['Fairy Tail', ['fairy tail']],
 300:       ['Ghibli', ['ghibli', 'mononoke', 'arrietty', 'kokuriko']],
 301:     ];
 302:

 304:       _buildFileira('Todos', animes),
 305:       if (filmesAnime.isNotEmpty) _buildFileira('Filmes de Anime', filmesAnime),
 306:       for (final f in franquias)
 307:         if (animes.any((o) => _combina(o, f[1] as List<String>)))
 308:           _buildFileira(f[0] as String, animes.where((o) => _combina(o, f[1] as List<String>)).toList()),
 309:     ];
 310:   }

 312:   Widget _buildBanner(List<ObraAgrupada> items) {
 313:     return SizedBox(
 314:       height: 230,
 315:       child: Stack(
 316:         children: [
 317:           PageView.builder(
 318:             controller: _bannerCtrl,
 319:             itemCount: items.length,
 320:             onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _bannerIdx = i),
 321:             itemBuilder: (_, i) {
 322:               final o = items[i];
 323:               final img = o.bannerUrl.isNotEmpty ? o.bannerUrl : o.capaUrl;
 324:               return GestureDetector(
 325:                 onTap: () => _abrir(o),
 326:                 child: Stack(
 327:                   fit: StackFit.expand,
 328:                   children: [
 329:                     if (img.isNotEmpty) Image.network(img, fit: BoxFit.cover, errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => Container(color: Colors.grey[900])),
 330:                     Container(decoration: const BoxDecoration(gradient: LinearGradient(begin: Alignment.topCenter, end: Alignment.bottomCenter, colors: [Colors.transparent, Colors.black87]))),
 331:                     Positioned(
 332:                       left: 16, right: 80, bottom: 18,
 333:                       child: Text(o.titulo.toUpperCase(), maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: 0.5)),
 334:                     ),
 335:                   ],
 336:                 ),
 337:               );
 338:             },
 339:           ),
 340:           Positioned(
 341:             right: 16, bottom: 20,
 342:             child: Row(
 343:               children: List.generate(items.length, (i) => Container(
 344:                 margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 5),
 345:                 width: 8, height: 8,
 346:                 decoration: BoxDecoration(shape: BoxShape.circle, color: _bannerIdx == i ? Colors.white : Colors.white38),
 347:               )),
 348:             ),
 349:           ),
 350:         ],
 351:       ),
 352:     );
 353:   }
 354: 
 355:   Widget _buildFileira(String titulo, List<ObraAgrupada> lista, {bool horizontal = false, bool iconeLive = false, bool mostrarProgresso = false, bool ranking = false}) {
 356:     final amostra = lista.take(10).toList();
 357:     final cardW = horizontal ? 165.0 : 112.0;
 358:     final cardH = horizontal ? 96.0 : 162.0;
 359: 
 360:     return Column(
 361:       crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 362:       children: [
 363:         Padding(
 364:           padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 18, 8, 10),
 365:           child: Row(
 366:             children: [
 367:               Text(titulo, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
 368:               if (iconeLive) const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 8), child: Icon(Icons.equalizer, color: Color(0xFFFFB800), size: 18)),
 369:               const Spacer(),
 370:               IconButton(
 371:                 icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: Colors.white70),
 372:                 onPressed: () => _verTodos(titulo, lista),
 373:                 padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
 374:                 constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
 375:               ),
 376:             ],
 377:           ),
 378:         ),
 379:         SizedBox(
 380:           height: cardH + 42,
 381:           child: ListView.builder(
 382:             scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
 383:             padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
 384:             itemCount: amostra.length,
 385:             itemBuilder: (_, i) {
 386:               final o = amostra[i];
 387:               final prog = _progresso[o.chaveGrupo];
 388:               final badgeEp = _etiquetaSerie(o);
 389:               return GestureDetector(
 390:                 onTap: () => _abrir(o),
 391:                 child: Container(
 392:                   width: cardW,
 393:                   margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 10),
 394:                   child: Column(
 395:                     crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 396:                     children: [
 397:                       ClipRRect(
 398:                         borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 399:                         child: Stack(
 400:                           children: [
 401:                             Container(
 402:                               width: cardW, height: cardH, color: const Color(0xFF1A1A1A),
 403:                               child: o.capaUrl.isNotEmpty
 404:                                   ? Image.network(o.capaUrl, fit: BoxFit.cover, errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => const Icon(Icons.movie, color: Colors.white24))
 405:                                   : const Icon(Icons.movie, color: Colors.white24),
 406:                             ),
 407:                             if (ranking)
 408:                               Positioned(top: 4, left: 4, child: Container(padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0xFFFFB800), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4)), child: Text('#${i + 1}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11)))),
 409:                             if (!mostrarProgresso && !horizontal && badgeEp.isNotEmpty)
 410:                               Positioned(
 411:                                 left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
 412:                                 child: Container(
 413:                                   padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 6),
 414:                                   decoration: const BoxDecoration(gradient: LinearGradient(begin: Alignment.topCenter, end: Alignment.bottomCenter, colors: [Colors.transparent, Colors.black87])),
 415:                                   child: Text(badgeEp, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
 416:                                 ),
 417:                               ),
 418:                             if (mostrarProgresso && prog != null)
 419:                               Positioned(
 420:                                 left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
 421:                                 child: Column(
 422:                                   children: [
 423:                                     if ((prog['etiqueta'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty)
 424:                                       Container(color: Colors.black54, padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 1), child: Text(prog['etiqueta'], style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
 425:                                     LinearProgressIndicator(value: (prog['progresso'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.2, minHeight: 3, backgroundColor: Colors.white24, valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation(Color(0xFFFFB800))),
 426:                                   ],
 427:                                 ),
 428:                               ),
 429:                           ],
 430:                         ),
 431:                       ),
 432:                       const SizedBox(height: 5),
 433:                       Text(o.titulo, maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12, height: 1.2)),
 434:                     ],
 435:                   ),
 436:                 ),
 437:               );
 438:             },
 439:           ),
 440:         ),
 441:       ],
 442:     );
 443:   }
 444: }

   9: class DetalheObraScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  10:   final ObraAgrupada obra;
  11:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogoCompleto;
  12:

  14:     super.key,
  15:     required this.obra,
  16:     this.catalogoCompleto = const [],
  17:   });
  18: 
  19:   @override
  20:   State<DetalheObraScreen> createState() => _DetalheObraScreenState();
  21: }
  22: 
  23: class _DetalheObraScreenState extends State<DetalheObraScreen> {
  24:   late ObraAgrupada _obraAtual;
  25:   bool _favorito = false;
  26:   int _temporadaSelecionada = 1;
  27:   String _audioSelecionado = '';
  28:   String _resolucaoSelecionada = '';
  29: 
  30:   @override
  31:   void initState() {
  32:     super.initState();
  33:     _obraAtual = widget.obra;
  34:     if (_obraAtual.temporadasDisponiveis.isNotEmpty) {
  35:       _temporadaSelecionada = _obraAtual.temporadasDisponiveis.first;
  36:     }
  37:     if (_obraAtual.audiosDisponiveis.isNotEmpty) {
  38:       _audioSelecionado = _obraAtual.audiosDisponiveis.first;
  39:     }
  40:     if (_obraAtual.resolucoesDisponiveis.isNotEmpty) {
  41:       _resolucaoSelecionada = _obraAtual.resolucoesDisponiveis.first;
  42:     }
  43:     _carregarDetalhesExtras();
  44:     CdnStreamService.preAquecerMagnet(_escolherMelhorFonte()?.urlStream);
  45:   }
  46: 
  47:   Future<void> _carregarDetalhesExtras() async {
  48:     final favs = await SupabaseService.carregarFavoritos();
  49:     if (mounted) {
  50:       setState(() => _favorito = favs.contains(_obraAtual.chaveGrupo));
  51:     }
  52:     final enriquecido = await SupabaseService.enriquecerComTmdb(_obraAtual);
  53:     if (mounted) {
  54:       setState(() => _obraAtual = enriquecido);
  55:       CdnStreamService.preAquecerMagnet(_escolherMelhorFonte()?.urlStream);
  56:     }
  57:   }
  58: 
  59:   Future<void> _alternarFavorito() async {
  60:     final novo = await SupabaseService.alternarFavorito(_obraAtual.chaveGrupo);
  61:     if (mounted) {
  62:       setState(() => _favorito = novo);
  63:       ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
  64:         SnackBar(
  65:           duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
  66:           content: Text(novo ? 'Adicionado aos Meus Favoritos!' : 'Removido dos Favoritos.'),
  67:         ),
  68:       );
  69:     }
  70:   }
  71: 
  72:   FonteMidia? _escolherMelhorFonte([int? epNumero]) {
  73:     if (_obraAtual.fontes.isEmpty) return null;
  74:     var candidatas = _obraAtual.fontes;
  75:     if (_obraAtual.isSerie) {
  76:       candidatas = candidatas.where((f) => (f.temporada ?? 1) == _temporadaSelecionada).toList();
  77:       if (epNumero != null) {
  78:         candidatas = candidatas.where((f) => f.episodio == epNumero).toList();
  79:       }
  80:     }
  81:     if (candidatas.isEmpty) candidatas = _obraAtual.fontes;
  82: 
  83:     final matchExato = candidatas.where((f) =>
  84:         (_audioSelecionado.isEmpty || f.tipoAudio.toLowerCase().contains(_audioSelecionado.toLowerCase())) &&
  85:         (_resolucaoSelecionada.isEmpty || f.resolucao.toLowerCase().contains(_resolucaoSelecionada.toLowerCase())));
  86:     if (matchExato.isNotEmpty) return matchExato.first;
  87: 
  88:     final matchAudio = candidatas.where((f) =>
  89:         _audioSelecionado.isEmpty || f.tipoAudio.toLowerCase().contains(_audioSelecionado.toLowerCase()));
  90:     if (matchAudio.isNotEmpty) return matchAudio.first;
  91: 
  92:     return candidatas.first;
  93:   }
  94: 
  95:   void _reproduzir([FonteMidia? fonteEspecifica]) {
  96:     final alvo = fonteEspecifica ?? _escolherMelhorFonte();
  97:     if (alvo == null) {
  98:       ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
  99:         const SnackBar(content: Text('Este título do Top 100 ainda está em breve no catálogo.')),
 100:       );
 101:       return;
 102:     }
 103:     final eps = _obraAtual.isSerie ? _obraAtual.episodiosDaTemporada(_temporadaSelecionada) : <FonteMidia>[];
 104:     Navigator.push(
 105:       context,
 106:       MaterialPageRoute(
 107:         builder: (_) => CinemaPlayerScreen(
 108:           obra: _obraAtual,
 109:           fonteInicial: alvo,
 110:           listaEpisodios: eps,
 111:         ),
 112:       ),
 113:     );
 114:   }
 115: 
 116:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _relacionados {
 117:     if (widget.catalogoCompleto.isEmpty) return [];
 118:     final genPrincipal = _obraAtual.genero.split(',').first.trim().toLowerCase();
 119:     return widget.catalogoCompleto
 120:         .where((o) =>
 121:             o.chaveGrupo != _obraAtual.chaveGrupo &&
 122:             (genPrincipal.isEmpty || o.genero.toLowerCase().contains(genPrincipal)))
 123:         .take(12)
 124:         .toList();
 125:   }
