  31: class _CinemaPlayerScreenState extends State<CinemaPlayerScreen> {
  32:   late final Player _player;
  33:   late final VideoController _videoCtrl;
  34:   late FonteMidia _fonteAtual;
  35: 
  36:   bool _controlesVisiveis = true;
  37:   bool _tentouBackup = false;
  38:   bool _carregandoStream = true;
  39:   String? _erroPlayer;
  40:   Timer? _hideTimer;
  41:   Timer? _saveProgressTimer;
  42: 
  43:   Duration _posicao = Duration.zero;
  44:   Duration _duracao = Duration.zero;
  45:   bool _tocando = true;
  46: 
  47:   // Configurações de Legenda
  48:   bool _legendaAtiva = true;
  49:   double _tamanhoLegenda = 28.0;
  50:   Color _corLegenda = Colors.white;
  51:   String _legendaSelecionada = 'Nenhuma';
  52: 
  53:   int _retryAutoCount = 0;
  54:   String? _osdTexto;
  55:   IconData? _osdIcone;
  56:   Timer? _osdTimer;
  57:   final FocusNode _playerRootFocus = FocusNode();
  58: 
  59:   @override
  60:   void initState() {
  61:     super.initState();
  62:     CinemaOrientation.emPlayer = true;
  63:     _fonteAtual = widget.fonteInicial;
  64:     SystemChrome.setEnabledSystemUIMode(SystemUiMode.immersiveSticky);
  65:     SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations([
  66:       DeviceOrientation.landscapeLeft,
  67:       DeviceOrientation.landscapeRight,
  68:     ]);
  69: 
  70:     _player = Player(
  71:       configuration: const PlayerConfiguration(
  72:         bufferSize: 20 * 1024 * 1024,
  73:       ),
  74:     );
  75:     if (_player.platform is NativePlayer) {
  76:       final np = _player.platform as NativePlayer;
  77:       np.setProperty('demuxer-lavf-o', 'reconnect=1,reconnect_streamed=1,reconnect_delay_max=5');
  78:       np.setProperty('network-timeout', '30');
  79:     }
  80:     _videoCtrl = VideoController(_player);
  81: 
  82:     _player.stream.position.listen((p) {
  83:       if (mounted) setState(() => _posicao = p);
  84:     });
  85:     _player.stream.duration.listen((d) {
  86:       if (mounted) setState(() => _duracao = d);
  87:     });
  88:     _player.stream.playing.listen((pl) {
  89:       if (mounted) setState(() => _tocando = pl);
  90:     });
  91:     _player.stream.buffering.listen((b) {
  92:       if (mounted && !b) setState(() => _carregandoStream = false);
  93:     });
  94:     _player.stream.error.listen((err) {
  95:       if (!mounted) return;
  96:       if (_retryAutoCount < 1 && (_fonteAtual.urlStream.toLowerCase().startsWith('magnet:') || _fonteAtual.urlStream.toLowerCase().endsWith('.torrent'))) {
  97:         _retryAutoCount++;
  98:         Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 1400), () {
  99:           if (mounted) _abrirMidia(_fonteAtual.urlStream);
 100:         });
 101:         return;
 102:       }
 103:       if (!_tentouBackup && _fonteAtual.urlStreamBackup.isNotEmpty) {
 104:         _tentouBackup = true;
 105:         _abrirMidia(_fonteAtual.urlStreamBackup);
 106:       } else {
 107:         setState(() {
 108:           _carregandoStream = false;
 109:           _erroPlayer = 'Falha ao reproduzir stream: $err';
 110:         });
 111:       }
 112:     });
 113: 
 114:     _iniciarFonte(_fonteAtual);
 115:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 116: 
 117:     _saveProgressTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 12), (_) {
 118:       _salvarProgressoAtual();
 119:     });
 120:   }
 121: 
 122:   Future<void> _iniciarFonte(FonteMidia fonte) async {
 123:     setState(() {
 124:       _fonteAtual = fonte;
 125:       _tentouBackup = false;
 126:       _carregandoStream = true;
 127:       _erroPlayer = null;
 128:     });
 129:     SupabaseService.registrarVisualizacao(fonte.id);
 130:     await _abrirMidia(fonte.urlStream);
 131:   }
 132: 
 133:   Future<void> _abrirMidia(String url) async {
 134:     try {
 135:       String urlFinal = url.trim();
 136:       final lower = urlFinal.toLowerCase();
 137:       if (lower.startsWith('magnet:?') || lower.endsWith('.torrent')) {
 138:         CdnResolucaoInfo? cdnInfo;
 139:         if (lower.startsWith('magnet:?')) {
 140:           cdnInfo = await CdnStreamService.resolverMagnet(urlFinal);
 141:         }
 142: 
 143:         if (!mounted) return;
 144: 
 145:         if (cdnInfo != null && cdnInfo.url.isNotEmpty) {
 146:           urlFinal = cdnInfo.url;
 147:         } else {
 148:           final swP2p = Stopwatch()..start();
 149:           final info = await TorrServerService.prepararMagnet(
 150:             urlFinal,
 151:             cancelado: () => !mounted,
 152:           );
 153:           swP2p.stop();
 154:           if (!mounted) return;
 155:           if (info == null) {
 156:             setState(() {
 157:               _carregandoStream = false;
 158:               _erroPlayer = TorrServerService.ultimoErro.isNotEmpty
 159:                   ? TorrServerService.ultimoErro
 160:                   : 'Não foi possível carregar o stream no momento.';
 161:             });
 162:             return;
 163:           }
 164:           CdnStreamService.ultimoDiagnostico =
 165:               'Origem: P2P Local (TorrServer) | ${swP2p.elapsedMilliseconds}ms';
 166:           urlFinal = info.playUrl;
 167:         }
 168:       }
 169: 
 170:       final headers = Map<String, String>.from(_fonteAtual.headers);
 171:       if (!headers.containsKey('User-Agent')) {
 172:         headers['User-Agent'] = 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36';
 173:       }
 174:       try {
 175:         final platform = _player.platform;
 176:         if (platform is NativePlayer) {
 177:           await platform.setProperty('network-timeout', '30');
 178:           await platform.setProperty('demuxer-lavf-o', 'reconnect=1,reconnect_streamed=1,reconnect_delay_max=5');
 179:           await platform.setProperty('cache', 'yes');
 180:           await platform.setProperty('demuxer-max-bytes', '33554432');
 181:         }
 182:       } catch (_) {}
 183:       await _player.open(Media(urlFinal, httpHeaders: headers), play: true);
 184:       await _player.play();
 185:     } catch (e) {
 186:       if (mounted) {
 187:         setState(() {
 188:           _carregandoStream = false;
 189:           _erroPlayer = e.toString();
 190:         });
 191:       }
 192:     }
 193:   }
 194: 
 195:   Future<void> _salvarProgressoAtual() async {
 196:     if (_duracao.inSeconds <= 10) return;
 197:     final pct = _posicao.inSeconds / _duracao.inSeconds;
 198:     await SupabaseService.salvarProgressoAssistido(
 199:       chaveGrupo: widget.obra.chaveGrupo,
 200:       progresso: pct,
 201:       etiquetaEp: _fonteAtual.etiquetaEpisodio,
 202:     );
 203:   }
 204: 
 205:   void _reiniciarTimerControles() {
 206:     _hideTimer?.cancel();
 207:     _osdTimer?.cancel();
 208:     _playerRootFocus.dispose();
 209:     setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = true);
 210:     _hideTimer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 5), () {
 211:       if (mounted && _tocando) {
 212:         setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = false);
 213:       }
 214:     });
 215:   }
 216: 
 217:   FonteMidia? get _proximoEpisodio {
 218:     if (widget.listaEpisodios.isEmpty) return null;
 219:     final idx = widget.listaEpisodios.indexWhere((e) => e.id == _fonteAtual.id);
 220:     if (idx >= 0 && idx + 1 < widget.listaEpisodios.length) {
 221:       return widget.listaEpisodios[idx + 1];
 222:     }
 223:     return null;
 224:   }
 225: 
 226:   void _pularAbertura() {
 227:     final salto = Duration(seconds: widget.obra.aberturaSegundos);
 228:     _player.seek(_posicao + salto);
 229:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 230:   }
 231: 
 232:   String _formatarTempo(Duration d) {
 233:     final h = d.inHours;
 234:     final m = d.inMinutes.remainder(60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
 235:     final s = d.inSeconds.remainder(60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
 236:     return h > 0 ? '$h:$m:$s' : '$m:$s';
 237:   }
 238: 
 239:   @override
 240:   void dispose() {
 241:     CinemaOrientation.emPlayer = false;
 242:     _salvarProgressoAtual();
 243:     _hideTimer?.cancel();
 244:     _saveProgressTimer?.cancel();
 245:     _player.dispose();
 246:     SystemChrome.setEnabledSystemUIMode(SystemUiMode.edgeToEdge);
 247:     SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations(DeviceOrientation.values);
 248:     super.dispose();
 249:   }
 250: 
 251:   void _abrirModalLegendas() {
 252:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 253:     final buscaCtrl = TextEditingController(text: widget.obra.titulo);
 254:     List<Map<String, dynamic>> resultadosSub = [];
 255:     bool carregandoSub = true;
 256:     bool jaBuscou = false;
 257:     bool iniciouAuto = false;
 258: 
 259:     showModalBottomSheet(
 260:       context: context,
 261:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF141822),
 262:       isScrollControlled: true,
 263:       builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
 264:         builder: (ctx, setModal) {
 265:           Future<String?> descobrirImdbId(String titulo) async {
 266:             try {
 267:               final limpo = titulo.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\(\d{4}\)'), '').trim();
 268:               if (limpo.isEmpty) return null;
 269:               final firstChar = limpo[0].toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]'), 'x');
 270:               final u = Uri.parse('https://v3.sg.media-imdb.com/suggestion/$firstChar/${Uri.encodeComponent(limpo)}.json');
 271:               final r = await http.get(u, headers: {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 272:               if (r.statusCode == 200) {
 273:                 final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(r.bodyBytes));
 274:                 final d = (data is Map ? data['d'] : null) as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
 275:                 for (final item in d) {
 276:                   if (item is Map) {
 277:                     final id = (item['id'] ?? '').toString();
 278:                     if (id.startsWith('tt')) return id;
 279:                   }
 280:                 }
 281:               }
 282:             } catch (_) {}
 283:             return null;
 284:           }
 285: 
 286:           Future<void> buscarMultiBancos() async {
 287:             final q = buscaCtrl.text.trim();
 288:             if (q.isEmpty) return;
 289:             setModal(() {
 290:               carregandoSub = true;
 291:               jaBuscou = true;
 292:               resultadosSub = [];
 293:             });
 294:             final encontrados = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
 295: 
 296:             final imdbId = await descobrirImdbId(q);
 297:             final temp = _fonteAtual.temporada ?? 1;
 298:             final ep = _fonteAtual.episodio ?? 1;
 299: 
 300:             // 1. OpenSubtitles V3 via IMDb ID (retorna .srt/.vtt diretos em PT/EN/ES)
 301:             if (imdbId != null && imdbId.startsWith('tt')) {
 302:               try {
 303:                 final tipo = widget.obra.isSerie ? 'series' : 'movie';
 304:                 final idQuery = widget.obra.isSerie ? '$imdbId:$temp:$ep' : imdbId;
 305:                 final uOs3 = Uri.parse('https://opensubtitles-v3.strem.io/subtitles/$tipo/$idQuery.json');
 306:                 final rOs3 = await http.get(uOs3).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 307:                 if (rOs3.statusCode == 200) {
 308:                   final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(rOs3.bodyBytes));
 309:                   final subs = (data is Map ? data['subtitles'] : null) as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
 310:                   for (final s in subs) {
 311:                     if (s is Map && (s['url'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty) {
 312:                       final lang = (s['lang'] ?? 'por').toString();
 313:                       final langLow = lang.toLowerCase();
 314:                       if (langLow.contains('por') || langLow.contains('pob') || langLow == 'pt' || langLow.contains('eng') || langLow.contains('spa')) {
 315:                         encontrados.add({
 316:                           'SubFileName': '${widget.obra.titulo} (${lang.toUpperCase()}) [OpenSubtitles]',
 317:                           'LanguageName': lang.toUpperCase(),
 318:                           'SubDownloadLink': s['url'].toString(),
 319:                           'Prioridade': (langLow.contains('por') || langLow.contains('pob') || langLow == 'pt') ? 0 : 1,
 320:                         });
 321:                       }
 322:                     }
 323:                   }
 324:                 }
 325:               } catch (_) {}
 326: 
 327:               // 2. Wyzie / SubSource API via IMDb ID (legendas SRT limpas)
 328:               try {
 329:                 final uWy = widget.obra.isSerie
 330:                     ? Uri.parse('https://sub.wyzie.ru/search?id=$imdbId&season=$temp&episode=$ep')
 331:                     : Uri.parse('https://sub.wyzie.ru/search?id=$imdbId');
 332:                 final rWy = await http.get(uWy).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 333:                 if (rWy.statusCode == 200) {
 334:                   final list = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(rWy.bodyBytes));
 335:                   if (list is List) {
 336:                     for (final s in list) {
 337:                       if (s is Map && (s['url'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty) {
 338:                         final lang = (s['display'] ?? s['language'] ?? 'PT').toString();
 339:                         final langLow = lang.toLowerCase();
 340:                         encontrados.add({
 341:                           'SubFileName': '${s['release'] ?? widget.obra.titulo} ($lang) [SubSource]',
 342:                           'LanguageName': lang,
 343:                           'SubDownloadLink': s['url'].toString(),
 344:                           'Prioridade': (langLow.contains('portug') || langLow == 'pt' || langLow == 'pb') ? 0 : 1,
 345:                         });
 346:                       }
 347:                     }
 348:                   }
 349:                 }
 350:               } catch (_) {}
 351:             }
 352: 
 353:             // Ordena colocando Portugues no topo
 354:             encontrados.sort((a, b) => (a['Prioridade'] as int).compareTo(b['Prioridade'] as int));
 355: 
 356:             if (ctx.mounted) {
 357:               setModal(() {
 358:                 resultadosSub = encontrados.take(15).toList();
 359:                 carregandoSub = false;
 360:               });
 361:             }
 362:           }
 363: 
 364:           if (!iniciouAuto) {
 365:             iniciouAuto = true;
 366:             Future.microtask(buscarMultiBancos);
 367:           }
 368: 
 369:           return Padding(
 370:             padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, MediaQuery.of(ctx).viewInsets.bottom + 20),
 371:             child: SizedBox(
 372:               height: 380,
 373:               child: Column(
 374:                 crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 375:                 children: [
 376:                   Row(
 377:                     children: [
 378:                       const Expanded(
 379:                         child: Text(
 380:                           'Legendas Online (OpenSubtitles & SubSource)',
 381:                           style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 15.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 382:                         ),
 383:                       ),
 384:                       Switch(
 385:                         value: _legendaAtiva,
 386:                         activeColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 387:                         onChanged: (v) {
 388:                           setState(() => _legendaAtiva = v);
 389:                           setModal(() {});
 390:                           if (!v) _player.setSubtitleTrack(SubtitleTrack.no());
 391:                         },
 392:                       ),
 393:                     ],
 394:                   ),
 395:                   Row(
 396:                     children: [
 397:                       const Text('Tamanho:', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
 398:                       Expanded(
 399:                         child: Slider(
 400:                           value: _tamanhoLegenda,
 401:                           min: 18,
 402:                           max: 44,
 403:                           activeColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 404:                           onChanged: (v) {
 405:                             setState(() => _tamanhoLegenda = v);
 406:                             setModal(() {});
 407:                           },
 408:                         ),
 409:                       ),
 410:                       ...[Colors.white, const Color(0xFFF5B82E), Colors.cyanAccent].map(
 411:                         (c) => GestureDetector(
 412:                           onTap: () {
 413:                             setState(() => _corLegenda = c);
 414:                             setModal(() {});
 415:                           },
 416:                           child: Container(
 417:                             width: 24,
 418:                             height: 24,
 419:                             margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
 420:                             decoration: BoxDecoration(
 421:                               color: c,
 422:                               shape: BoxShape.circle,
 423:                               border: Border.all(color: _corLegenda == c ? Colors.redAccent : Colors.white54, width: 2),
 424:                             ),
 425:                           ),
 426:                         ),
 427:                       ),
 428:                     ],
 429:                   ),
 430:                   const SizedBox(height: 8),
 431:                   Row(
 432:                     children: [
 433:                       Expanded(
 434:                         child: TextField(
 435:                           controller: buscaCtrl,
 436:                           style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13.5),
 437:                           decoration: InputDecoration(
 438:                             hintText: 'Título do filme ou série...',
 439:                             filled: true,
 440:                             fillColor: Colors.white10,
 441:                             isDense: true,
 442:                             border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
 443:                           ),
 444:                         ),
 445:                       ),
 446:                       const SizedBox(width: 8),
 447:                       ElevatedButton.icon(
 448:                         style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E), foregroundColor: Colors.black),
 449:                         onPressed: buscarMultiBancos,
 450:                         icon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 18),
 451:                         label: const Text('Buscar'),
 452:                       ),
 453:                     ],
 454:                   ),
 455:                   const SizedBox(height: 10),
 456:                   Expanded(
 457:                     child: carregandoSub
 458:                         ? const Center(
 459:                             child: Column(
 460:                               mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 461:                               children: [
 462:                                 CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 463:                                 SizedBox(height: 10),
 464:                                 Text('Buscando legendas em Português...', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13)),
 465:                               ],
 466:                             ),
 467:                           )
 468:                         : resultadosSub.isEmpty
 469:                             ? Center(
 470:                                 child: Column(
 471:                                   mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 472:                                   children: [
 473:                                     if (jaBuscou)
 474:                                       Container(
 475:                                         margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
 476:                                         padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
 477:                                         decoration: BoxDecoration(
 478:                                           color: Colors.redAccent.withOpacity(0.16),
 479:                                           borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 480:                                           border: Border.all(color: Colors.redAccent.withOpacity(0.5)),
 481:                                         ),
 482:                                         child: const Row(
 483:                                           mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 484:                                           children: [
 485:                                             Icon(Icons.subtitles_off, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 18),
 486:                                             SizedBox(width: 8),
 487:                                             Text(
 488:                                               'Legenda não encontrada para este título.',
 489:                                               style: TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13),
 490:                                             ),
 491:                                           ],
 492:                                         ),
 493:                                       ),
 494:                                     Text(
 495:                                       'Legenda ativa: $_legendaSelecionada\nTente buscar pelo título original em inglês se necessário.',
 496:                                       textAlign: TextAlign.center,
 497:                                       style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 12.5),
 498:                                     ),
 499:                                   ],
 500:                                 ),
 501:                               )
 502:                             : Column(
 503:                                 crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 504:                                 children: [
 505:                                   Container(
 506:                                     margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
 507:                                     padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
 508:                                     decoration: BoxDecoration(
 509:                                       color: Colors.greenAccent.withOpacity(0.14),
 510:                                       borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 511:                                       border: Border.all(color: Colors.greenAccent.withOpacity(0.5)),
 512:                                     ),
 513:                                     child: Row(
 514:                                       children: [
 515:                                         const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.greenAccent, size: 17),
 516:                                         const SizedBox(width: 8),
 517:                                         Text(
 518:                                           'Legenda encontrada! (${resultadosSub.length} opções)',
 519:                                           style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.greenAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12.5),
 520:                                         ),
 521:                                       ],
 522:                                     ),
 523:                                   ),
 524:                                   Expanded(
 525:                                     child: ListView.builder(
 526:                                       itemCount: resultadosSub.length,
 527:                                       itemBuilder: (_, i) {
 528:                                         final item = resultadosSub[i];
 529:                                         final nome = (item['SubFileName'] ?? 'Legenda').toString();
 530:                                         final idioma = (item['LanguageName'] ?? '').toString();
 531:                                         final dl = (item['SubDownloadLink'] ?? '').toString();
 532:                                         return ListTile(
 533:                                           dense: true,
 534:                                           leading: const Icon(Icons.subtitles, color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 535:                                           title: Text(nome, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13)),
 536:                                           subtitle: Text(idioma, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 11)),
 537:                                           onTap: () async {
 538:                                             if (dl.isNotEmpty) {
 539:                                               await _player.setSubtitleTrack(SubtitleTrack.uri(dl, title: nome, language: idioma));
 540:                                               setState(() {
 541:                                                 _legendaAtiva = true;
 542:                                                 _legendaSelecionada = nome;
 543:                                               });
 544:                                             }
 545:                                             if (ctx.mounted) Navigator.pop(ctx);
 546:                                           },
 547:                                         );
 548:                                       },
 549:                                     ),
 550:                                   ),
 551:                                 ],
 552:                               ),
 553:                   ),
 554:                 ],
 555:               ),
 556:             ),
 557:           );
 558:         },
 559:       ),
 560:     );
 561:   }
 562: 
 563:   void _abrirModalFontes() {
 564:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 565:     final fontesMesmoEp = widget.obra.fontes.where((f) {
 566:       if (_fonteAtual.episodio != null) {
 567:         return f.temporada == _fonteAtual.temporada && f.episodio == _fonteAtual.episodio;
 568:       }
 569:       return true;
 570:     }).toList();
 571: 
 572:     showModalBottomSheet(
 573:       context: context,
 574:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF141822),
 575:       builder: (ctx) => ListView(
 576:         padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
 577:         children: [
 578:           const Text('Resolução e Áudio', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 579:           const SizedBox(height: 12),
 580:           ...fontesMesmoEp.map(
 581:             (f) => ListTile(
 582:               leading: Icon(
 583:                 f.id == _fonteAtual.id ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.play_circle_outline,
 584:                 color: f.id == _fonteAtual.id ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white54,
 585:               ),
 586:               title: Text(f.badgeResumo, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 587:               subtitle: Text(f.formato.toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
 588:               onTap: () {
 589:                 Navigator.pop(ctx);
 590:                 _iniciarFonte(f);
 591:               },
 592:             ),
 593:           ),
 594:         ],
 595:       ),
 596:     );
 597:   }
 598: 
 599:   void _abrirModalEpisodios() {
 600:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 601:     showModalBottomSheet(
 602:       context: context,
 603:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF141822),
 604:       builder: (ctx) => ListView.builder(
 605:         padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
 606:         itemCount: widget.listaEpisodios.length,
 607:         itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
 608:           final ep = widget.listaEpisodios[idx];
 609:           final atual = ep.id == _fonteAtual.id;
 610:           return ListTile(
 611:             leading: Text(
 612:               ep.etiquetaEpisodio.isNotEmpty ? ep.etiquetaEpisodio : 'Ep ${idx + 1}',
 613:               style: TextStyle(color: atual ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white70, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 614:             ),
 615:             title: Text(ep.titulo, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
 616:             trailing: Text(ep.resolucao, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 12)),
 617:             onTap: () {
 618:               Navigator.pop(ctx);
 619:               _iniciarFonte(ep);
 620:             },
 621:           );
 622:         },
 623:       ),
 624:     );
 625:   }
 626: 
 627:   void _mostrarOsd(IconData icone, String texto) {
 628:     _osdTimer?.cancel();
 629:     if (!mounted) return;
 630:     setState(() {
 631:       _osdIcone = icone;
 632:       _osdTexto = texto;
 633:     });
 634:     _osdTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 1200), () {
 635:       if (mounted) {
 636:         setState(() {
 637:           _osdTexto = null;
 638:           _osdIcone = null;
 639:         });
 640:       }
 641:     });
 642:   }
 643: 
 644:   void _alternarPlayPause() {
 645:     _player.playOrPause();
 646:     final vaiPausar = _tocando;
 647:     _mostrarOsd(vaiPausar ? Icons.pause_circle_filled : Icons.play_circle_filled, vaiPausar ? 'Pausado' : 'Reproduzindo');
 648:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 649:   }
 650: 
 651:   void _buscarRelativo(int segundos) {
 652:     final alvo = _posicao + Duration(seconds: segundos);
 653:     final clamped = alvo < Duration.zero
 654:         ? Duration.zero
 655:         : (_duracao > Duration.zero && alvo > _duracao ? _duracao : alvo);
 656:     _player.seek(clamped);
 657:     _mostrarOsd(
 658:       segundos < 0 ? Icons.replay_10 : Icons.forward_10,
 659:       segundos < 0 ? '${segundos}s' : '+${segundos}s',
 660:     );
 661:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 662:   }
 663: 
 664:   bool _isConfirmKey(LogicalKeyboardKey k) {
 665:     return k == LogicalKeyboardKey.select ||
 666:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter ||
 667:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.numpadEnter ||
 668:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.accept ||
 669:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.gameButtonA ||
 670:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.space ||
 671:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlayPause;
 672:   }
 673: 
 674:   @override
 675:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 676:     final podePularAbertura = widget.obra.aberturaSegundos > 0 &&
 677:         _posicao.inSeconds >= 5 &&
 678:         _posicao.inSeconds <= (widget.obra.aberturaSegundos + 45);
 679:     final prox = _proximoEpisodio;
 680: 
 681:     return Scaffold(
 682:       backgroundColor: Colors.black,
 683:       body: Focus(
 684:         focusNode: _playerRootFocus,
 685:         autofocus: true,
 686:         onKeyEvent: (node, event) {
 687:           if (event is! KeyDownEvent) return KeyEventResult.ignored;
 688:           final k = event.logicalKey;
 689: 
 690:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlayPause ||
 691:               k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlay ||
 692:               k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPause) {
 693:             _alternarPlayPause();
 694:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 695:           }
 696:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaFastForward) {
 697:             _buscarRelativo(10);
 698:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 699:           }
 700:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaRewind) {
 701:             _buscarRelativo(-10);
 702:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 703:           }
 704: 
 705:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.escape || k == LogicalKeyboardKey.goBack) {
 706:             if (_controlesVisiveis && !_carregandoStream) {
 707:               setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = false);
 708:               _playerRootFocus.requestFocus();
 709:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 710:             }
 711:             Navigator.pop(context);
 712:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 713:           }
 714: 
 715:           if (!_controlesVisiveis) {
 716:             if (_isConfirmKey(k)) {
 717:               _alternarPlayPause();
 718:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 719:             }
 720:             if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowRight) {
 721:               _buscarRelativo(10);
 722:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 723:             }
 724:             if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowLeft) {
 725:               _buscarRelativo(-10);
 726:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 727:             }
 728:             if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp || k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown) {
 729:               _reiniciarTimerControles();
 730:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 731:             }
 732:           } else {
 733:             _reiniciarTimerControles();
 734:             if (_playerRootFocus.hasPrimaryFocus) {
 735:               if (_isConfirmKey(k)) {
 736:                 _alternarPlayPause();
 737:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 738:               }
 739:               if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowRight) {
 740:                 _buscarRelativo(10);
 741:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 742:               }
 743:               if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowLeft) {
 744:                 _buscarRelativo(-10);
 745:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 746:               }
 747:               if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown || k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp) {
 748:                 FocusScope.of(context).nextFocus();
 749:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 750:               }
 751:             }
 752:           }
 753:           return KeyEventResult.ignored;
 754:         },
 755:         child: GestureDetector(
 756:           onTap: () {
 757:             if (_controlesVisiveis && !_carregandoStream) {
 758:               setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = false);
 759:               _playerRootFocus.requestFocus();
 760:             } else {
 761:               _reiniciarTimerControles();
 762:             }
 763:           },
 764:           child: Stack(
 765:             fit: StackFit.expand,
 766:             children: [
 767:               Video(
 768:                 controller: _videoCtrl,
 769:                 controls: NoVideoControls,
 770:                 subtitleViewConfiguration: SubtitleViewConfiguration(
 771:                   visible: _legendaAtiva,
 772:                   style: TextStyle(
 773:                     fontSize: _tamanhoLegenda,
 774:                     color: _corLegenda,
 775:                     fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
 776:                     backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
 777:                   ),
 778:                 ),
 779:               ),
 780:               if (_carregandoStream)
 781:                 const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E))),
 782:               if (_osdTexto != null && !_carregandoStream)
 783:                 Center(
 784:                   child: IgnorePointer(
 785:                     child: Container(
 786:                       padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 22, vertical: 14),
 787:                       decoration: BoxDecoration(
 788:                         color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.78),
 789:                         borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
 790:                         border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E), width: 1.5),
 791:                       ),
 792:                       child: Row(
 793:                         mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 794:                         children: [
 795:                           if (_osdIcone != null) Icon(_osdIcone, color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E), size: 32),
 796:                           const SizedBox(width: 10),
 797:                           Text(
 798:                             _osdTexto!,
 799:                             style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 800:                           ),
 801:                         ],
 802:                       ),
 803:                     ),
 804:                   ),
 805:                 ),
 806:               if (_erroPlayer != null)
 807:                 Center(
 808:                   child: Container(
 809:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
 810:                     decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black87, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
 811:                     child: Column(
 812:                       mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 813:                       children: [
 814:                         const Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 42),
 815:                         const SizedBox(height: 10),
 816:                         Text(_erroPlayer!, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
 817:                         const SizedBox(height: 12),
 818:                         CinemaFocusBox(
 819:                           autofocus: true,
 820:                           onTap: () => _iniciarFonte(_fonteAtual),
 821:                           child: Container(
 822:                             padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 10),
 823:                             decoration: BoxDecoration(
 824:                               color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 825:                               borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 826:                             ),
 827:                             child: const Text('Tentar novamente', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
 828:                           ),
 829:                         ),
 830:                       ],
 831:                     ),
 832:                   ),
 833:                 ),
 834:               if (podePularAbertura)
 835:                 Positioned(
 836:                   right: 28,
 837:                   bottom: 92,
 838:                   child: CinemaFocusBox(
 839:                     onTap: _pularAbertura,
 840:                     child: Container(
 841:                       padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 12),
 842:                       decoration: BoxDecoration(
 843:                         color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 844:                         borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 845:                       ),
 846:                       child: Row(
 847:                         mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 848:                         children: [
 849:                           const Icon(Icons.fast_forward, color: Colors.black),
 850:                           const SizedBox(width: 6),
 851:                           Text(
 852:                             'Pular Abertura (+${widget.obra.aberturaSegundos}s)',
 853:                             style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
 854:                           ),
 855:                         ],
 856:                       ),
 857:                     ),
 858:                   ),
 859:                 ),
 860:               if (_controlesVisiveis && !_carregandoStream)
 861:                 Container(
 862:                   decoration: const BoxDecoration(
 863:                     gradient: LinearGradient(
 864:                       begin: Alignment.topCenter,
 865:                       end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
 866:                       colors: [Colors.black87, Colors.transparent, Colors.transparent, Colors.black87],
 867:                     ),
 868:                   ),
 869:                   child: Column(
 870:                     children: [
 871:                       Padding(
 872:                         padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, 8),
 873:                         child: Row(
 874:                           children: [
 875:                             CinemaFocusBox(
 876:                               borderRadius: 24,
 877:                               onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context),
 878:                               child: const Padding(
 879:                                 padding: EdgeInsets.all(8),
 880:                                 child: Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white, size: 26),
 881:                               ),
 882:                             ),
 883:                             const SizedBox(width: 10),
 884:                             Expanded(
 885:                               child: Column(
 886:                                 crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 887:                                 children: [
 888:                                   Text(
 889:                                     widget.obra.titulo,
 890:                                     maxLines: 1,
 891:                                     overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
 892:                                     style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 893:                                   ),
 894:                                   Text(
 895:                                     '${_fonteAtual.etiquetaEpisodio} ${_fonteAtual.badgeResumo}'.trim(),
 896:                                     style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E), fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
 897:                                   ),
 898:                                 ],
 899:                               ),
 900:                             ),
 901:                           ],
 902:                         ),
 903:                       ),
 904:                       const Spacer(),
 905:                       Row(
 906:                         mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
 907:                         children: [
 908:                           CinemaFocusBox(
 909:                             borderRadius: 30,
 910:                             scaleOnFocus: 1.12,
 911:                             onTap: () => _buscarRelativo(-10),
 912:                             child: const Padding(
 913:                               padding: EdgeInsets.all(10),
 914:                               child: Icon(Icons.replay_10, color: Colors.white, size: 38),
 915:                             ),
 916:                           ),
 917:                           const SizedBox(width: 28),
 918:                           CinemaFocusBox(
 919:                             autofocus: true,
 920:                             borderRadius: 36,
 921:                             scaleOnFocus: 1.12,
 922:                             onTap: _alternarPlayPause,
 923:                             child: CircleAvatar(
 924:                               radius: 30,
 925:                               backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 926:                               child: Icon(_tocando ? Icons.pause : Icons.play_arrow, size: 34, color: Colors.black),
 927:                             ),
 928:                           ),
 929:                           const SizedBox(width: 28),
 930:                           CinemaFocusBox(

  49:   void _atualizarPopulares() {
  50:     final ordenados = List<ObraAgrupada>.from(widget.catalogo)
  51:       ..sort((a, b) => b.scorePopularidade.compareTo(a.scorePopularidade));
  52:     final top = ordenados.take(30).toList()..shuffle();
  53:     setState(() => _populares = top.take(10).toList());
  54:   }
  55: 
  56:   Future<void> _executarBusca() async {
  57:     final q = _ctrl.text.trim();
  58:     if (q.isEmpty) {
  59:       setState(() => _resultados = []);
  60:       return;
  61:     }
  62:     setState(() => _buscando = true);
  63:     final res = await SupabaseService.buscarComElencoEDiretores(q, widget.catalogo);
  64:     if (mounted) {
  65:       setState(() {
  66:         _resultados = res;
  67:         _buscando = false;
  68:       });
  69:     }
  70:   }
  71: 
  72:   void _digitar(String char) {
  73:     _ctrl.text = '${_ctrl.text}$char';
  74:     _executarBusca();
  75:   }
  76: 
  77:   void _apagarUltimo() {
  78:     if (_ctrl.text.isNotEmpty) {
  79:       _ctrl.text = _ctrl.text.substring(0, _ctrl.text.length - 1);
  80:       _executarBusca();
  81:     }
  82:   }
  83: 
  84:   void _limpar() {
  85:     _ctrl.clear();
  86:     setState(() => _resultados = []);
  87:   }

 369:   Widget _teclaEspecial({String? label, IconData? icon, required VoidCallback onTap}) {
 370:     return CinemaFocusBox(
 371:       onTap: onTap,
 372:       borderRadius: 6,
 373:       scaleOnFocus: 1.07,

 378:             ? Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))
 379:             : Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 19),
 380:       ),

 385: class TvFiltroScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 386:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 387:   final String tipoInicial;
 388:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 389: 
 390:   const TvFiltroScreen({
 391:     super.key,
 392:     required this.catalogo,
 393:     this.tipoInicial = 'Filmes',
 394:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 395:   });
 396: 
 397:   @override
 398:   State<TvFiltroScreen> createState() => _TvFiltroScreenState();
 399: }
 400: 
 401: class _TvFiltroScreenState extends State<TvFiltroScreen> {
 402:   late String _tipo;
 403:   String _genero = 'Todos';
 404:   String _ano = 'Todos';
 405:   String _pais = 'Todos';
 406:   String _audio = 'Todos';
 407: 
 408:   static const List<String> _tipos = ['Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'];
 409:   static const List<String> _generos = [
 410:     'Todos', 'Ação', 'Drama', 'Aventura', 'Crime',
 411:     'Ficção científica', 'Desenho', 'Comédia', 'Romance',
 412:     'Animação', 'Suspense', 'Terror', 'Mistério',
 413:   ];
 414:   static const List<String> _anos = [
 415:     'Todos', '2026', '2025', '2024', '2023', '2022',
 416:     '2021', '2020', '2019', '2018', '2017', 'Anteriores',
 417:   ];
 418:   static const List<String> _paises = [
 419:     'Todos', 'Brasil', 'Portugal', 'Estados Unidos', 'México',
 420:     'Reino Unido', 'Japão', 'Coreia do Sul', 'Espanha', 'Alemanha',
 421:   ];
 422:   static const List<String> _audios = ['Todos', 'Português', 'Espanhol', 'Inglês'];
 423: 
 424:   @override
 425:   void initState() {
 426:     super.initState();
 427:     _tipo = widget.tipoInicial;
 428:   }
 429: 
 430:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _filtrados {
 431:     return widget.catalogo.where((o) {
 432:       final cat = o.categoria.toLowerCase();
 433:       final gen = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 434:       final tags = o.tags.map((t) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(t)).toList();
 435: 
 436:       if (_tipo == 'Filmes' && (!cat.contains('filme') || cat.contains('série') || cat.contains('serie'))) return false;
 437:       if (_tipo == 'Séries' && !(cat.contains('série') || cat.contains('serie'))) return false;
 438:       if (_tipo == 'Kids' && !(cat.contains('infantil') || cat.contains('kids') || gen.contains('infantil') || gen.contains('animacao') || tags.contains('kids'))) return false;
 439:       if (_tipo == 'Anime' && !(cat.contains('anime') || gen.contains('anime') || tags.contains('anime'))) return false;
 440: 
 441:       if (_genero != 'Todos') {
 442:         final gBusca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_genero);
 443:         if (!gen.contains(gBusca) && !tags.any((t) => t.contains(gBusca))) return false;
 444:       }
 445: 
 446:       if (_ano != 'Todos') {
 447:         if (_ano == 'Anteriores') {
 448:           final y = int.tryParse(o.ano.trim()) ?? 9999;
 449:           if (y >= 2017) return false;
 450:         } else if (!o.ano.contains(_ano)) {
 451:           return false;
 452:         }
 453:       }
 454: 
 455:       if (_pais != 'Todos') {
 456:         final pNorm = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.pais);
 457:         final pBusca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_pais);
 458:         if (pNorm.isNotEmpty && !pNorm.contains(pBusca)) return false;
 459:       }
 460: 
 461:       if (_audio != 'Todos') {
 462:         final aBusca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_audio);
 463:         final temAudio = o.audiosDisponiveis.any((a) {
 464:           final an = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(a);
 465:           if (aBusca == 'portugues') return an.contains('portugues') || an.contains('dublado') || an.contains('nacional') || an == 'pt';
 466:           if (aBusca == 'ingles') return an.contains('ingles') || an.contains('legendado') || an == 'en';
 467:           return an.contains(aBusca);
 468:         });
 469:         if (!temAudio) return false;
 470:       }
 471:       return true;
 472:     }).toList();
 473:   }
 474: 
 475:   Widget _linhaFiltro({required String titulo, required List<String> opcoes, required String selecionado, required void Function(String) onSelect}) {
 476:     return SizedBox(
 477:       height: 36,
 478:       child: Row(
 479:         children: [
 480:           SizedBox(width: 72, child: Text('$titulo:', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
 481:           Expanded(
 482:             child: ListView.separated(
 483:               scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
 484:               itemCount: opcoes.length,
 485:               separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 8),
 486:               itemBuilder: (_, i) {
 487:                 final item = opcoes[i];
 488:                 final ativo = item == selecionado;
 489:                 return CinemaFocusBox(
 490:                   onTap: () => onSelect(item),
 491:                   borderRadius: 18,
 492:                   child: Container(
 493:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 6),
 494:                     decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.transparent, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
 495:                     alignment: Alignment.center,
 496:                     child: Text(item, style: TextStyle(color: ativo ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: ativo ? FontWeight.w800 : FontWeight.w500)),
 497:                   ),
 498:                 );
 499:               },
 500:             ),
 501:           ),
 502:         ],
 503:       ),
 504:     );
 505:   }
 506: 
 507:   @override
 508:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 509:     final lista = _filtrados;
 510:     return Scaffold(
 511:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0E141B),
 512:       appBar: AppBar(
 513:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 514:         title: const Text('Filtro Avançado', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 515:         actions: [
 516:           Padding(
 517:             padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 20),
 518:             child: Center(child: Text('${lista.length} encontrados', style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E), fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
 519:           ),
 520:         ],
 521:       ),
 522:       body: Padding(
 523:         padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 6, 24, 16),
 524:         child: Column(
 525:           children: [
 526:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Tipo', opcoes: _tipos, selecionado: _tipo, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _tipo = v)),
 527:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 528:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Gênero', opcoes: _generos, selecionado: _genero, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _genero = v)),
 529:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 530:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Ano', opcoes: _anos, selecionado: _ano, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _ano = v)),
 531:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 532:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'País', opcoes: _paises, selecionado: _pais, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _pais = v)),
 533:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 534:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Áudio', opcoes: _audios, selecionado: _audio, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _audio = v)),
 535:             const SizedBox(height: 16),
 536:             Expanded(
 537:               child: lista.isEmpty
 538:                   ? const Center(child: Text('Nenhum título encontrado com essa combinação de filtros.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 16)))
 539:                   : GridView.builder(
 540:                       gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(maxCrossAxisExtent: 168, childAspectRatio: 0.62, crossAxisSpacing: 14, mainAxisSpacing: 14),
 541:                       itemCount: lista.length,
 542:                       itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(obra: lista[idx], onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(lista[idx])),
 543:                     ),
 544:             ),
 545:           ],
 546:         ),
 547:       ),
 548:     );
 549:   }
 550: }
 551: 
 552: class TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 553:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 554:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 555: 
 556:   const TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen({
 557:     super.key,
 558:     required this.catalogo,
 559:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 560:   });
 561: 
 562:   @override
 563:   State<TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen> createState() => _TvHistoricoFavoritosScreenState();
 564: }
 565: 
 566: class _TvHistoricoFavoritosScreenState extends State<TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen> {
 567:   int _abaLateral = 0;
 568:   bool _somenteSeries = false;
 569:   bool _modoEdicao = false;
 570:   final Set<String> _selecionados = {};
 571:   Map<String, Map<String, dynamic>> _historicoMap = {};
 572:   Set<String> _favoritosSet = {};
 573: 
 574:   @override
 575:   void initState() {
 576:     super.initState();
 577:     _carregarDados();
 578:   }
 579: 
 580:   Future<void> _carregarDados() async {
 581:     final hist = await SupabaseService.carregarProgressoAssistido();
 582:     final favs = await SupabaseService.carregarFavoritos();
 583:     if (mounted) {
 584:       setState(() {
 585:         _historicoMap = hist;
 586:         _favoritosSet = favs;
 587:       });
 588:     }
 589:   }
 590: 
 591:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _itensAtuais {
 592:     final base = _abaLateral == 0
 593:         ? widget.catalogo.where((o) => _historicoMap.containsKey(o.chaveGrupo)).toList()
 594:         : widget.catalogo.where((o) => _favoritosSet.contains(o.chaveGrupo)).toList();
 595:     return base.where((o) => _somenteSeries ? o.isSerie : !o.isSerie).toList();
 596:   }
 597: 
 598:   Future<void> _excluirSelecionados() async {
 599:     if (_selecionados.isEmpty) return;
 600:     if (_abaLateral == 0) {
 601:       await SupabaseService.removerDoHistorico(_selecionados.toList());
 602:     } else {
 603:       for (final ch in _selecionados) {
 604:         await SupabaseService.alternarFavorito(ch);
 605:       }
 606:     }
 607:     setState(() {
 608:       _selecionados.clear();
 609:       _modoEdicao = false;
 610:     });
 611:     await _carregarDados();
 612:   }
 613: 
 614:   Future<void> _limparTudo() async {
 615:     final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
 616:       context: context,
 617:       builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
 618:         backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF161B24),
 619:         title: Text(_abaLateral == 0 ? 'Limpar Histórico?' : 'Limpar Favoritos?'),
 620:         content: const Text('Tem certeza que deseja remover todos os itens desta lista?'),
 621:         actions: [
 622:           TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('Cancelar')),
 623:           ElevatedButton(
 624:             style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent),
 625:             onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
 626:             child: const Text('Excluir tudo'),
 627:           ),
 628:         ],
 629:       ),
 630:     );
 631:     if (ok == true) {
 632:       if (_abaLateral == 0) {
 633:         await SupabaseService.limparTodoHistorico();
 634:       } else {
 635:         for (final ch in _favoritosSet.toList()) {
 636:           await SupabaseService.alternarFavorito(ch);
 637:         }
 638:       }
 639:       setState(() {
 640:         _selecionados.clear();
 641:         _modoEdicao = false;
 642:       });
 643:       await _carregarDados();
 644:     }
 645:   }
 646: 
 647:   @override
 648:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 649:     final baseAtual = _abaLateral == 0
 650:         ? widget.catalogo.where((o) => _historicoMap.containsKey(o.chaveGrupo)).toList()
 651:         : widget.catalogo.where((o) => _favoritosSet.contains(o.chaveGrupo)).toList();
 652:     final totalFilmes = baseAtual.where((o) => !o.isSerie).length;
 653:     final totalSeries = baseAtual.where((o) => o.isSerie).length;
 654:     final exibidos = _itensAtuais;
 655: 
 656:     return Scaffold(
 657:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0E141B),
 658:       appBar: AppBar(
 659:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 660:         title: Text(_abaLateral == 0 ? 'Histórico' : 'Meus Favoritos'),
 661:       ),
 662:       body: Row(
 663:         children: [
 664:           Container(
 665:             width: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width < 420 ? 96 : (MediaQuery.of(context).size.width < 700 ? 112 : 230),
 666:             padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20),
 667:             color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.24),
 668:             child: Column(
 669:               crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
 670:               children: [
 671:                 _itemMenuLateral(
 672:                   titulo: 'Histórico',
 673:                   ativo: _abaLateral == 0,
 674:                   onTap: () => setState(() {
 675:                     _abaLateral = 0;
 676:                     _modoEdicao = false;
 677:                     _selecionados.clear();
 678:                   }),
 679:                 ),
 680:                 const SizedBox(height: 8),
 681:                 _itemMenuLateral(
 682:                   titulo: 'Meus Favoritos',
 683:                   ativo: _abaLateral == 1,
 684:                   onTap: () => setState(() {
 685:                     _abaLateral = 1;
 686:                     _modoEdicao = false;
 687:                     _selecionados.clear();
 688:                   }),
 689:                 ),
 690:               ],
 691:             ),
 692:           ),
 693:           Expanded(
 694:             child: Padding(
 695:               padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 8, 24, 16),
 696:               child: Column(
 697:                 crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 698:                 children: [
 699:                   Row(
 700:                     children: [
 701:                       _pilulaTipo(label: 'Filmes', ativo: !_somenteSeries, onTap: () => setState(() => _somenteSeries = false)),
 702:                       const SizedBox(width: 12),
 703:                       _pilulaTipo(label: 'Séries', ativo: _somenteSeries, onTap: () => setState(() => _somenteSeries = true)),
 704:                       const SizedBox(width: 24),
 705:                       Container(
 706:                         padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
 707:                         decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white10, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
 708:                         child: Row(
 709:                           children: [
 710:                             IconButton(
 711:                               tooltip: 'Selecionar itens para remover',
 712:                               icon: Icon(Icons.edit_note, color: _modoEdicao ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white),
 713:                               onPressed: () => setState(() {
 714:                                 _modoEdicao = !_modoEdicao;
 715:                                 _selecionados.clear();
 716:                               }),
 717:                             ),
 718:                             IconButton(
 719:                               tooltip: 'Excluir tudo',
 720:                               icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.white),
 721:                               onPressed: _limparTudo,
 722:                             ),
 723:                           ],
 724:                         ),
 725:                       ),
 726:                       if (_modoEdicao && _selecionados.isNotEmpty) ...[
 727:                         const SizedBox(width: 12),
 728:                         ElevatedButton.icon(
 729:                           style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent),
 730:                           onPressed: _excluirSelecionados,
 731:                           icon: const Icon(Icons.delete, size: 18),
 732:                           label: Text('Remover (${_selecionados.length})'),
 733:                         ),
 734:                       ],
 735:                       const Spacer(),
 736:                       Column(
 737:                         crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
 738:                         children: [
 739:                           Text('Total de filmes  $totalFilmes', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 740:                           Text('Total de séries  $totalSeries', style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E), fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 741:                         ],
 742:                       ),
 743:                     ],
 744:                   ),
 745:                   const SizedBox(height: 18),
 746:                   Expanded(
 747:                     child: exibidos.isEmpty
 748:                         ? Center(
 749:                             child: Text(
 750:                               _abaLateral == 0 ? 'Nenhum título assistido ainda.' : 'Nenhum título nos seus favoritos ainda.',
 751:                               style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 16),
 752:                             ),
 753:                           )
 754:                         : GridView.builder(
 755:                             gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(
 756:                               maxCrossAxisExtent: 168,
 757:                               childAspectRatio: 0.62,
 758:                               crossAxisSpacing: 14,
 759:                               mainAxisSpacing: 14,
 760:                             ),
 761:                             itemCount: exibidos.length,
 762:                             itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
 763:                               final obra = exibidos[idx];
 764:                               final prog = _historicoMap[obra.chaveGrupo]?['progresso'] as double?;
 765:                               final sel = _selecionados.contains(obra.chaveGrupo);
 766:                               return TvPosterCard(
 767:                                 obra: obra,
 768:                                 progresso: _abaLateral == 0 ? prog : null,
 769:                                 modoEdicao: _modoEdicao,
 770:                                 selecionadoEdicao: sel,
 771:                                 onTap: () {
 772:                                   if (_modoEdicao) {
 773:                                     setState(() {
 774:                                       if (sel) {
 775:                                         _selecionados.remove(obra.chaveGrupo);
 776:                                       } else {
 777:                                         _selecionados.add(obra.chaveGrupo);
 778:                                       }
 779:                                     });
 780:                                   } else {
 781:                                     widget.onAbrirObra(obra);
 782:                                   }
 783:                                 },
 784:                               );
 785:                             },
 786:                           ),
 787:                   ),
 788:                 ],
 789:               ),
 790:             ),
 791:           ),
 792:         ],
 793:       ),
 794:     );
 795:   }
 796: 
 797:   Widget _itemMenuLateral({required String titulo, required bool ativo, required VoidCallback onTap}) {
 798:     return CinemaFocusBox(
 799:       onTap: onTap,
 800:       borderRadius: 8,
 801:       scaleOnFocus: 1.02,
 802:       child: Container(
 803:         padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 14),
 804:         decoration: BoxDecoration(
 805:           gradient: ativo ? const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFFF5B82E), Color(0x22F5B82E)]) : null,
 806:         ),
 807:         child: Text(titulo, style: TextStyle(color: ativo ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 808:       ),
 809:     );
 810:   }
 811: 
 812:   Widget _pilulaTipo({required String label, required bool ativo, required VoidCallback onTap}) {
 813:     return CinemaFocusBox(
 814:       onTap: onTap,
 815:       borderRadius: 20,
 816:       child: Container(
 817:         padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 22, vertical: 8),
 818:         decoration: BoxDecoration(
 819:           color: ativo ? Colors.white24 : Colors.transparent,
 820:           borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
 821:           border: Border.all(color: ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white30),
 822:         ),
 823:         child: Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 824:       ),
 825:     );
 826:   }
 827: }

 829: class TvTop100RankingScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 830:   final String fonte;
 831:   final String categoriaInicial;
 832:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 833:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 834: 
 835:   const TvTop100RankingScreen({
 836:     super.key,
 837:     required this.fonte,
 838:     this.categoriaInicial = 'Filmes',
 839:     required this.catalogo,
 840:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 841:   });
 842: 
 843:   @override
 844:   State<TvTop100RankingScreen> createState() => _TvTop100RankingScreenState();
 845: }
 846: 
 847: class _TvTop100RankingScreenState extends State<TvTop100RankingScreen> {
 848:   late String _categoria;
 849:   bool _carregando = true;
 850:   List<ObraAgrupada> _lista = [];
 851:   static const List<String> _cats = ['Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'];
 852: 
 853:   @override
 854:   void initState() {
 855:     super.initState();
 856:     _categoria = widget.categoriaInicial;
 857:     _carregar();
 858:   }
 859: 
 860:   Future<void> _carregar() async {
 861:     setState(() => _carregando = true);
 862:     final res = await SupabaseService.carregarTop100Ranking(
 863:       fonte: widget.fonte,
 864:       categoria: _categoria,
 865:       catalogoLocal: widget.catalogo,
 866:     );
 867:     if (mounted) {
 868:       setState(() {
 869:         _lista = res;
 870:         _carregando = false;
 871:       });
 872:     }
 873:   }
 874: 
 875:   @override
 876:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 877:     final tituloBase = widget.fonte == 'imdb' ? 'Top 100 IMDb' : 'Top 100 TMDB';
 878:     return Scaffold(
 879:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0B0C10),
 880:       appBar: AppBar(
 881:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 882:         title: Text('$tituloBase • $_categoria', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 883:       ),
 884:       body: Column(
 885:         children: [
 886:           SizedBox(
 887:             height: 44,
 888:             child: ListView.separated(
 889:               padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
 890:               scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
 891:               itemCount: _cats.length,
 892:               separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 10),
 893:               itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
 894:                 final c = _cats[idx];
 895:                 final ativo = c == _categoria;
 896:                 return ChoiceChip(
 897:                   label: Text(c),
 898:                   selected: ativo,
 899:                   selectedColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 900:                   labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ativo ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
 901:                   onSelected: (_) {
 902:                     setState(() => _categoria = c);
 903:                     _carregar();
 904:                   },
 905:                 );
 906:               },
 907:             ),
 908:           ),
 909:           const SizedBox(height: 14),
 910:           Expanded(
 911:             child: _carregando
 912:                 ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)))
 913:                 : GridView.builder(
 914:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 4, 24, 20),
 915:                     gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(
 916:                       maxCrossAxisExtent: 168,
 917:                       childAspectRatio: 0.62,
 918:                       crossAxisSpacing: 14,
 919:                       mainAxisSpacing: 14,
 920:                     ),
 921:                     itemCount: _lista.length,
 922:                     itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(
 923:                       obra: _lista[idx],
 924:                       rankNumero: idx + 1,
 925:                       onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(_lista[idx]),
 926:                     ),
 927:                   ),
 928:           ),
 929:         ],
 930:       ),
 931:     );
 932:   }
 933: }

 935: class TvSubcategoriaScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 936:   final String titulo;
 937:   final List<ObraAgrupada> itens;
 938:   final List<String> subfiltros;
 939:   final String? filtroInicial;
 940:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 941:   final VoidCallback onAbrirBusca;
 942:   final VoidCallback onAbrirFiltro;
 943: 
 944:   const TvSubcategoriaScreen({
 945:     super.key,
 946:     required this.titulo,
 947:     required this.itens,
 948:     required this.subfiltros,
 949:     this.filtroInicial,
 950:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 951:     required this.onAbrirBusca,
 952:     required this.onAbrirFiltro,
 953:   });
 954: 
 955:   @override
 956:   State<TvSubcategoriaScreen> createState() => _TvSubcategoriaScreenState();
 957: }
 958: 
 959: class _TvSubcategoriaScreenState extends State<TvSubcategoriaScreen> {
 960:   bool _menuAberto = true;
 961:   late String _selecionado;
 962: 
 963:   @override
 964:   void initState() {
 965:     super.initState();
 966:     _selecionado = widget.filtroInicial ?? widget.subfiltros.first;
 967:   }
 968: 
 969:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _filtrados {
 970:     if (_selecionado == 'Todos' || _selecionado == 'Tudo') return widget.itens;
 971:     final busca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_selecionado);
 972:     return widget.itens.where((o) {
 973:       final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 974:       final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
 975:       final tit = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.titulo);
 976:       return g.contains(busca) || t.any((x) => x.contains(busca)) || tit.contains(busca);
 977:     }).toList();
 978:   }
 979: 
 980:   @override
 981:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 982:     final lista = _filtrados;
 983:     return Scaffold(
 984:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0B0C10),
 985:       appBar: AppBar(
 986:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 987:         leading: IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back), onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)),
 988:         title: Row(
 989:           children: [
 990:             IconButton(
 991:               tooltip: _menuAberto ? 'Recolher menu' : 'Expandir menu',
 992:               icon: Icon(_menuAberto ? Icons.menu_open : Icons.menu),
 993:               onPressed: () => setState(() => _menuAberto = !_menuAberto),
 994:             ),
 995:             const SizedBox(width: 6),
 996:             Text('${widget.titulo} • $_selecionado', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 997:           ],
 998:         ),
 999:         actions: [
1000:           PerfilChipTopButton(onTap: () => PerfisSelectionOverlay.abrir(context, onPerfilSelecionado: () { if (mounted) setState(() {}); })), const SizedBox(width: 6), MaxMicButton(onPressed: () => MaxAssistantModal.abrir(context)), const SizedBox(width: 6), TopIconeComTooltip(icon: Icons.playlist_add_rounded, label: 'Pedidos', onPressed: () => PedidosScreenModal.abrir(context)), const SizedBox(width: 6), TopIconeComTooltip(icon: Icons.search, label: 'Buscar', onPressed: widget.onAbrirBusca),
1001:           const SizedBox(width: 10),
1002:           TopIconeComTooltip(icon: Icons.filter_alt_outlined, label: 'Filtro', onPressed: widget.onAbrirFiltro),
1003:           const SizedBox(width: 20),
1004:         ],
1005:       ),
1006:       body: Row(
1007:         children: [
1008:           if (_menuAberto)
1009:             Container(
1010:               width: 210,
1011:               color: const Color(0xFF11141C),
1012:               child: ListView.builder(
1013:                 padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
1014:                 itemCount: widget.subfiltros.length,
1015:                 itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
1016:                   final f = widget.subfiltros[idx];
1017:                   final ativo = f == _selecionado;
1018:                   return ListTile(
1019:                     selected: ativo,
1020:                     selectedTileColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E).withOpacity(0.18),
1021:                     title: Text(
1022:                       f,
1023:                       style: TextStyle(color: ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white70, fontWeight: ativo ? FontWeight.w800 : FontWeight.w500),
1024:                     ),
1025:                     onTap: () => setState(() => _selecionado = f),
1026:                   );
1027:                 },
1028:               ),
1029:             ),
1030:           Expanded(
1031:             child: lista.isEmpty
1032:                 ? const Center(child: Text('Nenhum título nesta subcategoria.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)))
1033:                 : GridView.builder(
1034:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
1035:                     gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(
1036:                       maxCrossAxisExtent: 168,
1037:                       childAspectRatio: 0.62,
1038:                       crossAxisSpacing: 14,
1039:                       mainAxisSpacing: 14,
1040:                     ),
1041:                     itemCount: lista.length,
1042:                     itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(obra: lista[idx], onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(lista[idx])),
1043:                   ),
1044:           ),
1045:         ],
1046:       ),
1047:     );
1048:   }
1049: }

  42:   @override
  43:   void initState() {
  44:     super.initState();
  45:     _carregarProgresso();
  46:     Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {
  47:       if (mounted) AppUpdaterService.verificarAtualizacao(context, silencioso: true);
  48:     });
  49:     _autoPlayTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 6), (_) {
  50:       if (!mounted || _abaIndex != 0) return;
  51:       if (_bannerFilmesCtrl.hasClients) {
  52:         final nextF = (_idxBannerFilme + 1) % 4;
  53:         _bannerFilmesCtrl.animateToPage(nextF, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500), curve: Curves.easeInOut);
  54:       }
  55:       if (_bannerSeriesCtrl.hasClients) {
  56:         final nextS = (_idxBannerSerie + 1) % 4;
  57:         _bannerSeriesCtrl.animateToPage(nextS, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500), curve: Curves.easeInOut);
  58:       }
  59:     });
  60:   }
  61: 
  62:   Future<void> _carregarProgresso() async {
  63:     final p = await SupabaseService.carregarProgressoAssistido();
  64:     if (mounted) setState(() => _progressoMap = p);
  65:   }
  66: 
  67:   @override
  68:   void dispose() {
  69:     _autoPlayTimer?.cancel();
  70:     _bannerFilmesCtrl.dispose();
  71:     _bannerSeriesCtrl.dispose();
  72:     super.dispose();
  73:   }

  75:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _filmes => widget.catalogo
  76:       .where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('filme') && !o.isSerie)
  77:       .toList();
  78: 
  79:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _series =>
  80:       widget.catalogo.where((o) => o.isSerie).toList();
  81: 
  82:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _kids => widget.catalogo.where((o) {
  83:         final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
  84:         final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
  85:         final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
  86:         return c.contains('infantil') || c.contains('kids') || g.contains('infantil') || g.contains('animacao') || t.contains('kids');
  87:       }).toList();
  88: 
  89:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _animes => widget.catalogo.where((o) {
  90:         final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
  91:         final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
  92:         final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
  93:         return c.contains('anime') || g.contains('anime') || t.contains('anime');
  94:       }).toList();
  95: 
  96:   List<ObraAgrupada> _filtrarTagOuGenero(List<ObraAgrupada> base, String termo) {
  97:     final q = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(termo);
  98:     return base.where((o) {
  99:       final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 100:       final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
 101:       final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
 102:       return g.contains(q) || c.contains(q) || t.any((x) => x.contains(q));
 103:     }).toList();
 104:   }
