 391:   // =========================================================================
 392:   // TMDB: METADADOS COMPLETOS (DIRETOR, ELENCO, PAÍS, TÍTULO ORIGINAL)
 393:   // =========================================================================
 394:   static Future<ObraAgrupada> enriquecerComTmdb(ObraAgrupada obra) async {
 395:     if (obra.diretor.isNotEmpty &&
 396:         obra.elenco.isNotEmpty &&
 397:         obra.pais.isNotEmpty) {
 398:       return obra;
 399:     }
 400:     if (_cacheCreditosTmdb.containsKey(obra.chaveGrupo)) {
 401:       final c = _cacheCreditosTmdb[obra.chaveGrupo]!;
 402:       return obra.copyWith(
 403:         tituloOriginal: c['tituloOriginal'],
 404:         pais: c['pais'],
 405:         diretor: c['diretor'],
 406:         elenco: c['elenco'],
 407:         sinopse: c['sinopse'],
 408:         notaImdb: c['nota'],
 409:       );
 410:     }
 411: 
 412:     try {
 413:       final tipo = obra.isSerie ? 'tv' : 'movie';
 414:       int? idTmdb = obra.tmdbId;
 415: 
 416:       if (idTmdb == null) {
 417:         final searchUri = Uri.parse(
 418:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/search/$tipo?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&query=${Uri.encodeQueryComponent(obra.titulo)}');
 419:         final sResp =
 420:             await http.get(searchUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 421:         if (sResp.statusCode == 200) {
 422:           final results =
 423:               (jsonDecode(utf8.decode(sResp.bodyBytes))['results'] as List?) ??
 424:                   [];
 425:           if (results.isNotEmpty) {
 426:             idTmdb = results.first['id'] as int?;
 427:           }
 428:         }
 429:       }
 430: 
 431:       if (idTmdb != null) {
 432:         final detUri = Uri.parse(
 433:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/$tipo/$idTmdb?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&append_to_response=credits');
 434:         final dResp =
 435:             await http.get(detUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 436:         if (dResp.statusCode == 200) {
 437:           final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(dResp.bodyBytes));
 438:           final orig =
 439:               (data['original_title'] ?? data['original_name'] ?? '').toString();
 440:           final countries = (data['production_countries'] as List?) ?? [];
 441:           final paisNome = countries.isNotEmpty
 442:               ? (countries.first['name'] ?? '').toString()
 443:               : '';
 444:           final credits = data['credits'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
 445:           final cast = (credits['cast'] as List?) ?? [];
 446:           final crew = (credits['crew'] as List?) ?? [];
 447: 
 448:           final diretores = crew
 449:               .where((c) => (c['job'] ?? '') == 'Director')
 450:               .map((c) => (c['name'] ?? '').toString())
 451:               .where((n) => n.isNotEmpty)
 452:               .take(2)
 453:               .join(', ');
 454:           final criadores = ((data['created_by'] as List?) ?? [])
 455:               .map((c) => (c['name'] ?? '').toString())
 456:               .where((n) => n.isNotEmpty)
 457:               .take(2)
 458:               .join(', ');
 459:           final elencoStr = cast
 460:               .map((c) => (c['name'] ?? '').toString())
 461:               .where((n) => n.isNotEmpty)
 462:               .take(5)
 463:               .join(', ');
 464:           final sinopseTmdb = (data['overview'] ?? '').toString();
 465:           final vote = (data['vote_average'] is num)
 466:               ? (data['vote_average'] as num).toDouble()
 467:               : 0.0;
 468: 
 469:           final info = {
 470:             'tituloOriginal': orig,
 471:             'pais': paisNome,
 472:             'diretor': diretores.isNotEmpty ? diretores : criadores,
 473:             'elenco': elencoStr,
 474:             'sinopse': sinopseTmdb,
 475:             'nota': double.parse(vote.toStringAsFixed(1)),
 476:           };
 477:           _cacheCreditosTmdb[obra.chaveGrupo] = info;
 478:           return obra.copyWith(
 479:             tituloOriginal: orig,
 480:             pais: paisNome,
 481:             diretor: info['diretor'] as String,
 482:             elenco: elencoStr,
 483:             sinopse: sinopseTmdb,
 484:             notaImdb: info['nota'] as double,
 485:           );
 486:         }
 487:       }
 488:     } catch (_) {}
 489:     return obra;
 490:   }
 491: 
 492:   // =========================================================================
 493:   // BUSCA UNIFICADA (TÍTULOS + ELENCO + DIRETORES VIA TMDB)
 494:   // =========================================================================
 495:   static Future<List<ObraAgrupada>> buscarComElencoEDiretores(
 496:       String query, List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo) async {
 497:     final qNorm = normalizarTexto(query.trim());
 498:     if (qNorm.isEmpty) return [];
 499: 
 500:     final Map<String, ObraAgrupada> resultados = {};
 501:     for (final o in catalogo) {
 502:       final tit = normalizarTexto(o.titulo);
 503:       final orig = normalizarTexto(o.tituloOriginal);
 504:       final dir = normalizarTexto(o.diretor);
 505:       final el = normalizarTexto(o.elenco);
 506:       final gen = normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 507:       if (tit.contains(qNorm) ||
 508:           orig.contains(qNorm) ||
 509:           dir.contains(qNorm) ||
 510:           el.contains(qNorm) ||
 511:           gen.contains(qNorm)) {
 512:         resultados[o.chaveGrupo] = o;
 513:       }
 514:     }
 515: 
 516:     // Busca pessoa (ator/diretor) no TMDB para cruzar com o catálogo
 517:     if (qNorm.length >= 3) {
 518:       try {
 519:         final pUri = Uri.parse(
 520:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/search/person?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&query=${Uri.encodeQueryComponent(query.trim())}');
 521:         final resp = await http.get(pUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 522:         if (resp.statusCode == 200) {
 523:           final pessoas =
 524:               (jsonDecode(utf8.decode(resp.bodyBytes))['results'] as List?) ??
 525:                   [];
 526:           final Set<int> idsRelacionados = {};
 527:           final Set<String> titulosRelacionados = {};
 528: 
 529:           for (final p in pessoas.take(2)) {
 530:             final known = (p['known_for'] as List?) ?? [];
 531:             for (final k in known) {
 532:               if (k['id'] is int) idsRelacionados.add(k['id']);
 533:               final t1 = normalizarTexto((k['title'] ?? '').toString());
 534:               final t2 = normalizarTexto((k['name'] ?? '').toString());
 535:               if (t1.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t1);
 536:               if (t2.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t2);
 537:             }
 538:             if (p['id'] is int) {
 539:               final credUri = Uri.parse(
 540:                   'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/person/${p['id']}/combined_credits?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR');
 541:               final cResp =
 542:                   await http.get(credUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 543:               if (cResp.statusCode == 200) {
 544:                 final cData = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(cResp.bodyBytes));
 545:                 for (final item in [
 546:                   ...((cData['cast'] as List?) ?? []),
 547:                   ...((cData['crew'] as List?) ?? []),
 548:                 ]) {
 549:                   if (item['id'] is int) idsRelacionados.add(item['id']);
 550:                   final t1 = normalizarTexto((item['title'] ?? '').toString());
 551:                   final t2 = normalizarTexto((item['name'] ?? '').toString());
 552:                   if (t1.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t1);
 553:                   if (t2.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t2);
 554:                 }
 555:               }
 556:             }
 557:           }
 558: 
 559:           for (final o in catalogo) {
 560:             if ((o.tmdbId != null && idsRelacionados.contains(o.tmdbId)) ||
 561:                 titulosRelacionados.contains(normalizarTexto(o.titulo))) {
 562:               resultados[o.chaveGrupo] = o;
 563:             }
 564:           }
 565:         }
 566:       } catch (_) {}
 567:     }
 568: 
 569:     return resultados.values.toList();
 570:   }
 571: 
 572:   // =========================================================================
 573:   // TOP 100 IMDb & TMDB POR CATEGORIA (INCLUI TÍTULOS FORA DO CATÁLOGO)
 574:   // =========================================================================
 575:   static Future<List<ObraAgrupada>> carregarTop100Ranking({
 576:     required String fonte, // 'imdb' ou 'tmdb'
 577:     required String categoria, // 'Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'
 578:     required List<ObraAgrupada> catalogoLocal,
 579:   }) async {
 580:     final isTv = categoria == 'Séries' || categoria == 'Anime';
 581:     final endpoint = isTv ? 'tv' : 'movie';
 582:     final sort = fonte == 'imdb'
 583:         ? 'vote_average.desc&vote_count.gte=${isTv ? 400 : 1200}'
 584:         : 'popularity.desc';
 585: 
 586:     String extraGenre = '';
 587:     if (categoria == 'Kids') extraGenre = '&with_genres=10751,16';
 588:     if (categoria == 'Anime') {
 589:       extraGenre = '&with_genres=16&with_original_language=ja';
 590:     }
 591: 
 592:     final Map<String, ObraAgrupada> mapaLocalPorTitulo = {};
 593:     final Map<int, ObraAgrupada> mapaLocalPorId = {};
 594:     for (final o in catalogoLocal) {
 595:       mapaLocalPorTitulo[normalizarTexto(o.titulo)] = o;
 596:       if (o.tmdbId != null) mapaLocalPorId[o.tmdbId!] = o;
 597:     }
 598: 
 599:     final List<ObraAgrupada> ranking = [];
 600:     try {
 601:       // 5 páginas x 20 itens = Top 100
 602:       final futures = List.generate(5, (idx) {
 603:         final page = idx + 1;
 604:         final uri = Uri.parse(
 605:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/discover/$endpoint?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&sort_by=$sort$extraGenre&page=$page');
 606:         return http.get(uri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
 607:       });
 608: 
 609:       final responses = await Future.wait(futures);
 610:       for (final resp in responses) {
 611:         if (resp.statusCode != 200) continue;
 612:         final results =
 613:             (jsonDecode(utf8.decode(resp.bodyBytes))['results'] as List?) ?? [];
 614:         for (final item in results) {
 615:           if (ranking.length >= 100) break;
 616:           final int? id = item['id'] is int ? item['id'] : null;
 617:           final titulo =
 618:               (item['title'] ?? item['name'] ?? 'Sem Título').toString();
 619:           final normTit = normalizarTexto(titulo);
 620: 
 621:           // Se já existir no seu catálogo do Supabase, vincula com os streams reais
 622:           if (id != null && mapaLocalPorId.containsKey(id)) {
 623:             ranking.add(mapaLocalPorId[id]!);
 624:             continue;
 625:           }
 626:           if (mapaLocalPorTitulo.containsKey(normTit)) {
 627:             ranking.add(mapaLocalPorTitulo[normTit]!);
 628:             continue;
 629:           }
 630: 
 631:           final poster = (item['poster_path'] ?? '').toString();
 632:           final backdrop = (item['backdrop_path'] ?? '').toString();
 633:           final dataLanc =
 634:               (item['release_date'] ?? item['first_air_date'] ?? '').toString();
 635:           final ano = dataLanc.length >= 4 ? dataLanc.substring(0, 4) : '';
 636:           final nota = (item['vote_average'] is num)
 637:               ? double.parse((item['vote_average'] as num).toStringAsFixed(1))
 638:               : 0.0;
 639: 
 640:           ranking.add(ObraAgrupada(
 641:             chaveGrupo: 'rank_${fonte}_${categoria}_${id ?? ranking.length}',
 642:             tmdbId: id,
 643:             titulo: titulo,
 644:             tituloOriginal:
 645:                 (item['original_title'] ?? item['original_name'] ?? '')
 646:                     .toString(),
 647:             categoria: categoria,
 648:             genero: categoria,
 649:             sinopse: (item['overview'] ?? '').toString(),
 650:             capaUrl: poster.isNotEmpty
 651:                 ? 'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w500$poster'
 652:                 : '',
 653:             bannerUrl: backdrop.isNotEmpty
 654:                 ? 'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w1280$backdrop'
 655:                 : '',
 656:             ano: ano,
 657:             emDestaque: false,
 658:             ordemDestaque: ranking.length + 1,
 659:             notaImdb: nota,
 660:             fontes: const [], // Ainda não adicionado no catálogo (exibe badge "Em breve")
 661:           ));
 662:         }
 663:       }
 664:     } catch (_) {}
 665: 
 666:     return ranking;
 667:   }
 668: 
 669: }

  29:   static Process? _process;

  33:   static Future<bool> _portaAberta() async {
  34:     try {
  35:       final s = await Socket.connect('127.0.0.1', 8090, timeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 350));
  36:       s.destroy();
  37:       return true;
  38:     } catch (_) {
  39:       return false;
  40:     }
  41:   }
  42: 
  43:   static Future<void> _aplicarConfiguracoes() async {
  44:     if (_configurado) return;
  45:     try {
  46:       await _postJson('/settings', {
  47:         'action': 'set',
  48:         'sets': {
  49:           'CacheSize': 67108864,
  50:           'ReaderReadAHead': 95,
  51:           'PreloadCache': 2,
  52:           'UseDisk': false,
  53:           'ForceEncrypt': false,
  54:           'RetrackersMode': 1,
  55:           'TorrentDisconnectTimeout': 30,
  56:           'EnableIPv6': true,
  57:           'DisableTCP': false,
  58:           'DisableUTP': false,
  59:           'DisableUPNP': false,
  60:           'DisableDHT': false,
  61:           'DisablePEX': false,
  62:           'DisableUpload': false,
  63:           'ConnectionsLimit': 120,
  64:           'PeersListenPort': 0,
  65:         },
  66:       });
  67:       _configurado = true;
  68:     } catch (_) {}
  69:   }
  70: 
  71:   static Future<bool> garantirAtivo() async {
  72:     if (await _portaAberta()) {
  73:       await _aplicarConfiguracoes();
  74:       return true;
  75:     }
  76:     try {
  77:       final paths = await _channel.invokeMapMethod('getPaths');
  78:       final nativeLibDir = (paths?['nativeLibDir'] ?? '').toString();
  79:       final filesDir = (paths?['filesDir'] ?? '').toString();
  80:       final binPath = '$nativeLibDir/libtorrserver.so';
  81:       if (nativeLibDir.isEmpty || !await File(binPath).exists()) {
  82:         ultimoErro = 'Binario TorrServer nao encontrado.';
  83:         return false;
  84:       }
  85:       final dataDir = Directory('$filesDir/torrserver');
  86:       if (!await dataDir.exists()) await dataDir.create(recursive: true);
  87: 
  88:       final tentativas = [
  89:         ['-p', '8090', '-d', dataDir.path],
  90:         ['--port', '8090', '--path', dataDir.path],
  91:         ['']..clear(),
  92:       ];
  93: 
  94:       for (final args in tentativas) {
  95:         _process?.kill();
  96:         _process = await Process.start(
  97:           binPath,
  98:           args,
  99:           workingDirectory: dataDir.path,
 100:           environment: {
 101:             'HOME': filesDir,
 102:             'TMPDIR': dataDir.path,
 103:             'RES_OPTIONS': 'ndots:0',
 104:             'GODEBUG': 'netdns=go',
 105:           },
 106:         );
 107:         for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
 108:           await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
 109:           if (await _portaAberta()) {
 110:             ultimoErro = '';
 111:             await _aplicarConfiguracoes();
 112:             return true;
 113:           }
 114:         }
 115:       }
 116:       ultimoErro = 'Timeout na porta 8090.';
 117:       return false;
 118:     } catch (e) {
 119:       ultimoErro = 'Erro ao iniciar TorrServer: $e';
 120:       return false;
 121:     }
 122:   }
 123: 
 124:   static Future _postJson(String endpoint, Map body) async {
 125:     final c = HttpClient()..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 4);
 126:     try {
 127:       final req = await c.postUrl(Uri.parse('$baseUrl$endpoint'));
 128:       req.headers.contentType = ContentType.json;
 129:       req.write(jsonEncode(body));
 130:       final res = await req.close().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 131:       final txt = await utf8.decoder.bind(res).join();
 132:       if (txt.trim().startsWith('{')) return jsonDecode(txt);
 133:     } catch (_) {} finally {
 134:       c.close(force: true);
 135:     }
 136:     return null;
 137:   }
 138: 
 139:   static Future<List<int>?> _baixarTorrentCache(String hashHex) async {
 140:     final hUpper = hashHex.toUpperCase();
 141:     final urls = [
 142:       'https://itorrents.org/torrent/$hUpper.torrent',
 143:       'https://torrage.info/torrent.php?h=$hUpper',
 144:       'https://btcache.me/torrent/$hUpper',
 145:     ];
 146:     for (final u in urls) {
 147:       final c = HttpClient()..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 4);
 148:       try {
 149:         final req = await c.getUrl(Uri.parse(u));
 150:         req.headers.set('User-Agent', 'Mozilla/5.0');
 151:         final res = await req.close().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 152:         if (res.statusCode == 200) {
 153:           final bytes = await res.fold<List<int>>([], (prev, el) => prev..addAll(el));
 154:           if (bytes.length > 100 && bytes.first == 100) {
 155:             return bytes;
 156:           }
 157:         } else {
 158:           await res.drain();
 159:         }
 160:       } catch (_) {} finally {
 161:         c.close(force: true);
 162:       }
 163:     }
 164:     return null;
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   static Future<bool> _uploadTorrentParaServidor(List<int> bytes, String hashHex) async {
 168:     final c = HttpClient()..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 4);
 169:     try {
 170:       const boundary = '----TorrServerBoundary7MA4YWxkTrZu0gW';
 171:       final req = await c.postUrl(Uri.parse('$baseUrl/torrent/upload'));
 172:       req.headers.set('Content-Type', 'multipart/form-data; boundary=$boundary');
 173:       final header = '--$boundary\r\n'
 174:           'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="$hashHex.torrent"\r\n'
 175:           'Content-Type: application/x-bittorrent\r\n\r\n';
 176:       const footer = '\r\n--$boundary--\r\n';
 177:       req.add(utf8.encode(header));
 178:       req.add(bytes);
 179:       req.add(utf8.encode(footer));
 180:       final res = await req.close().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 181:       await res.drain();
 182:       return res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300;
 183:     } catch (_) {
 184:       return false;
 185:     } finally {
 186:       c.close(force: true);
 187:     }
 188:   }
 189: 
 190:   static String _base32ParaHex(String b32) {
 191:     const alpha = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567';
 192:     final clean = b32.toUpperCase().replaceAll('=', '');
 193:     int bits = 0, val = 0;
 194:     final bytes = [];
 195:     for (int i = 0; i < clean.length; i++) {
 196:       final idx = alpha.indexOf(clean[i]);
 197:       if (idx < 0) return b32.toLowerCase();
 198:       val = (val << 5) | idx;
 199:       bits += 5;
 200:       if (bits >= 8) {
 201:         bytes.add((val >> (bits - 8)) & 0xFF);
 202:         bits -= 8;
 203:       }
 204:     }
 205:     return bytes.map((b) => (b as int).toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0')).join();
 206:   }
 207: 
 208:   static String _extrairHash(String magnet) {
 209:     final m = RegExp(r'btih:([a-zA-Z0-9]{32,40})', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(magnet);
 210:     if (m == null) return '';
 211:     final raw = m.group(1)!;
 212:     return raw.length == 32 ? _base32ParaHex(raw) : raw.toLowerCase();
 213:   }
 214: 
 215:   static String _enriquecerMagnet(String magnet) {
 216:     var clean = magnet.trim().replaceFirstMapped(
 217:       RegExp(r'(btih:)([a-zA-Z2-7]{32})(?=&|$)', caseSensitive: false),
 218:       (m) => '${m.group(1)}${_base32ParaHex(m.group(2)!)}',
 219:     );
 220:     const trackers = [
 221:       'http://tracker.opentrackr.org:1337/announce',
 222:       'https://tracker.tamersunion.org:443/announce',
 223:       'https://tracker.loligirl.cn:443/announce',
 224:       'https://tracker.gbitt.info:443/announce',
 225:       'http://93.158.213.92:1337/announce',
 226:       'udp://tracker.opentrackr.org:1337/announce',
 227:       'udp://93.158.213.92:1337/announce',
 228:       'udp://open.stealth.si:80/announce',
 229:       'udp://tracker.torrent.eu.org:451/announce',
 230:     ];
 231:     final sb = StringBuffer(clean);
 232:     for (final tr in trackers) {
 233:       if (!clean.toLowerCase().contains(Uri.encodeComponent(tr).toLowerCase())) {
 234:         sb.write('&tr=${Uri.encodeComponent(tr)}');
 235:       }
 236:     }
 237:     return sb.toString();
 238:   }
 239: 
 240:   static Future<TorrentStreamInfo?> prepararMagnet(
 241:     String magnetUrl, {
 242:     void Function(String)? onStatus,
 243:     void Function(double)? onProgress,
 244:     bool Function()? cancelado,
 245:   }) async {
 246:     final cleanMagnet = _enriquecerMagnet(magnetUrl);
 247:     final hash = _extrairHash(cleanMagnet);
 248:     onProgress?.call(0.15);
 249:     onStatus?.call('Iniciando motor TorrServer...');
 250:     if (!await garantirAtivo()) return null;
 251:     if (cancelado != null && cancelado()) return null;
 252: 
 253:     onProgress?.call(0.28);
 254:     onStatus?.call('Buscando arquivo .torrent em cache web...');
 255:     bool subiuTorrent = false;
 256:     if (hash.length == 40) {
 257:       final torrentBytes = await _baixarTorrentCache(hash);
 258:       if (torrentBytes != null) {
 259:         subiuTorrent = await _uploadTorrentParaServidor(torrentBytes, hash);
 260:       }
 261:     }
 262: 
 263:     if (!subiuTorrent) {
 264:       await _postJson('/torrents', {
 265:         'action': 'add',
 266:         'link': cleanMagnet,
 267:         'title': 'Stream',
 268:         'save_to_db': false,
 269:       });
 270:     }
 271: 
 272:     int fileId = 1;
 273:     String fileName = 'video.mkv';
 274:     int fileSize = 0;
 275:     bool encontrouArquivo = false;
 276: 
 277:     for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
 278:       if (cancelado != null && cancelado()) return null;
 279:       await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
 280:       onProgress?.call((0.35 + (i * 0.014)).clamp(0.35, 0.88));
 281:       final info = await _postJson('/torrents', {'action': 'get', 'hash': hash});
 282:       final files = info is Map ? info['file_stats'] : null;
 283:       if (files is List && files.isNotEmpty) {
 284:         Map? maior;
 285:         for (final f in files) {
 286:           if (f is Map) {
 287:             final p = (f['path'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
 288:             final ehVid = p.endsWith('.mkv') || p.endsWith('.mp4') || p.endsWith('.avi') || p.endsWith('.ts');
 289:             if (ehVid && (maior == null || (f['length'] ?? 0) > (maior['length'] ?? 0))) {
 290:               maior = f;
 291:             }
 292:           }
 293:         }
 294:         maior ??= files.first as Map;
 295:         fileId = (maior['id'] is int) ? maior['id'] as int : 1;
 296:         fileName = (maior['path'] ?? 'video.mkv').toString().split('/').last;
 297:         fileSize = (maior['length'] is int) ? maior['length'] as int : 0;
 298:         encontrouArquivo = true;
 299:         break;
 300:       }
 301:       final peers = info is Map ? (info['active_peers'] ?? 0) : 0;
 302:       onStatus?.call(subiuTorrent
 303:           ? 'Carregando metadados do .torrent...'
 304:           : 'Buscando metadados via P2P (peers: $peers)...');
 305:     }
 306: 
 307:     if (!encontrouArquivo) {
 308:       ultimoErro = 'Torrent sem seeds ou metadados indisponiveis no momento.';
 309:       return null;
 310:     }
 311: 
 312:     onProgress?.call(0.92);
 313:     onStatus?.call('Iniciando reproducao de $fileName...');
 314:     return TorrentStreamInfo(
 315:       playUrl: '$baseUrl/play/$hash/$fileId?play',
 316:       fileName: fileName,
 317:       fileSizeBytes: fileSize,
 318:       activePeers: 1,
 319:       totalSeeds: 1,
 320:       downloadSpeedBytes: 0,
 321:     );
 322:   }
 323: }

 118: class _TopIconeComTooltipState extends State<TopIconeComTooltip> {
 119:   final LayerLink _layerLink = LayerLink();
 120:   OverlayEntry? _overlayEntry;
 121:   bool _focado = false;
 122:   Timer? _timer;
 123: 
 124:   void _removerOverlay() {
 125:     _overlayEntry?.remove();
 126:     _overlayEntry = null;
 127:   }
 128: 
 129:   void _mostrarOverlay() {
 130:     _removerOverlay();
 131:     _timer?.cancel();
 132:     if (!mounted) return;
 133:     final overlay = Overlay.maybeOf(context);
 134:     if (overlay == null) return;
 135:     _overlayEntry = OverlayEntry(
 136:       builder: (_) => Positioned(
 137:         width: 190,
 138:         child: CompositedTransformFollower(
 139:           link: _layerLink,
 140:           showWhenUnlinked: false,
 141:           targetAnchor: Alignment.bottomCenter,
 142:           followerAnchor: Alignment.topCenter,
 143:           offset: const Offset(0, 6),
 144:           child: IgnorePointer(
 145:             child: Material(
 146:               color: Colors.transparent,
 147:               child: Center(
 148:                 child: Container(
 149:                   padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
 150:                   decoration: BoxDecoration(
 151:                     color: const Color(0xFF141821).withOpacity(0.98),
 152:                     borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
 153:                     border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E).withOpacity(0.85)),
 154:                     boxShadow: const [
 155:                       BoxShadow(color: Colors.black87, blurRadius: 10, offset: Offset(0, 4)),
 156:                     ],
 157:                   ),
 158:                   child: Text(
 159:                     widget.label,
 160:                     textAlign: TextAlign.center,
 161:                     style: const TextStyle(
 162:                       color: Colors.white,
 163:                       fontSize: 11.5,
 164:                       fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
 165:                     ),
 166:                   ),
 167:                 ),
 168:               ),
 169:             ),
 170:           ),
 171:         ),
 172:       ),
 173:     );
 174:     overlay.insert(_overlayEntry!);
 175:     _timer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 2), () {
 176:       if (mounted) _removerOverlay();
 177:     });
 178:   }
 179: 
 180:   @override
 181:   void dispose() {
 182:     _timer?.cancel();
 183:     _removerOverlay();
 184:     super.dispose();
 185:   }
 186: 
 187:   @override
 188:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 189:     return CompositedTransformTarget(
 190:       link: _layerLink,
 191:       child: CinemaFocusBox(
 192:         onTap: widget.onPressed,
 193:         borderRadius: 24,
 194:         scaleOnFocus: 1.12,
 195:         onFocusChanged: (hasFocus) {
 196:           if (mounted) setState(() => _focado = hasFocus);
 197:           if (hasFocus) {
 198:             _mostrarOverlay();
 199:           } else {
 200:             _removerOverlay();
 201:           }
 202:         },
 203:         child: AnimatedContainer(
 204:           duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 160),
 205:           padding: const EdgeInsets.all(9),
 206:           decoration: BoxDecoration(
 207:             color: _focado
 208:                 ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E).withOpacity(0.24)
 209:                 : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08),
 210:             shape: BoxShape.circle,
 211:           ),
 212:           child: Icon(
 213:             widget.icon,
 214:             size: 20,
 215:             color: _focado ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white,
 216:           ),
 217:         ),
 218:       ),
 219:     );
 220:   }

 415: 
 416: class CinemaFocusBox extends StatefulWidget {
 417:   final Widget child;
 418:   final VoidCallback? onTap;
 419:   final double borderRadius;
 420:   final double scaleOnFocus;
 421:   final bool autofocus;
 422:   final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
 423:   final ValueChanged<bool>? onFocusChanged;
 424: 
 425:   const CinemaFocusBox({
 426:     super.key,
 427:     required this.child,
 428:     this.onTap,
 429:     this.borderRadius = 12,
 430:     this.scaleOnFocus = 1.045,
 431:     this.autofocus = false,
 432:     this.padding = EdgeInsets.zero,
 433:     this.onFocusChanged,
 434:   });
 435: 
 436:   @override
 437:   State<CinemaFocusBox> createState() => _CinemaFocusBoxState();
 438: }
 439: 
 440: class _CinemaFocusBoxState extends State<CinemaFocusBox> {
 441:   bool _focused = false;
 442:   bool _hovered = false;
 443:   DateTime _ultimoAcionamento = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
 444: 
 445:   bool get _ativo => _focused || _hovered;
 446: 
 447:   void _acionar() {
 448:     if (widget.onTap == null) return;
 449:     final agora = DateTime.now();
 450:     if (agora.difference(_ultimoAcionamento).inMilliseconds < 280) return;
 451:     _ultimoAcionamento = agora;
 452:     widget.onTap!();
 453:   }
 454: 
 455:   bool _isConfirmKey(LogicalKeyboardKey key) {
 456:     return key == LogicalKeyboardKey.select ||
 457:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter ||
 458:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.numpadEnter ||
 459:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.accept ||
 460:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.gameButtonA ||
 461:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.space ||
 462:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlayPause;
 463:   }
 464: 
 465:   @override
 466:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 467:     return MouseRegion(
 468:       cursor: widget.onTap != null ? SystemMouseCursors.click : MouseCursor.defer,
 469:       onEnter: (_) => setState(() => _hovered = true),
 470:       onExit: (_) => setState(() => _hovered = false),
 471:       child: Actions(
 472:         actions: <Type, Action<Intent>>{
 473:           ActivateIntent: CallbackAction<ActivateIntent>(
 474:             onInvoke: (_) {
 475:               _acionar();
 476:               return null;
 477:             },
 478:           ),
 479:           ButtonActivateIntent: CallbackAction<ButtonActivateIntent>(
 480:             onInvoke: (_) {
 481:               _acionar();
 482:               return null;
 483:             },
 484:           ),
 485:         },
 486:         child: Focus(
 487:           autofocus: widget.autofocus,
 488:           onFocusChange: (f) {
 489:             setState(() => _focused = f);
 490:             widget.onFocusChanged?.call(f);
 491:           },
 492:           onKeyEvent: (node, event) {
 493:             if (_isConfirmKey(event.logicalKey)) {
 494:               if (event is KeyDownEvent) {
 495:                 _acionar();
 496:               }
 497:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 498:             }
 499:             return KeyEventResult.ignored;
 500:           },
 501:           child: GestureDetector(
 502:             onTap: _acionar,
 503:             child: AnimatedScale(
 504:               scale: _ativo ? widget.scaleOnFocus : 1.0,
 505:               duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 140),
 506:               curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
 507:               child: AnimatedContainer(
 508:                 duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 140),
 509:                 padding: widget.padding,
 510:                 decoration: BoxDecoration(
 511:                   borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(widget.borderRadius),
 512:                   border: Border.all(
 513:                     color: _ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.transparent,
 514:                     width: _ativo ? 3.0 : 1.0,
 515:                   ),
 516:                   boxShadow: _ativo
 517:                       ? [
 518:                           BoxShadow(
 519:                             color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E).withOpacity(0.50),
 520:                             blurRadius: 16,
 521:                             spreadRadius: 1.5,
 522:                           ),
 523:                         ]
 524:                       : const [],
 525:                 ),
 526:                 child: widget.child,
 527:               ),
 528:             ),
 529:           ),
 530:         ),
 531:       ),
 532:     );
 533:   }
 534: }

  69:     );
  70:   }

  76: class _PerfisSelectionOverlayState extends State<PerfisSelectionOverlay> {
  77:   Future<void> _selecionar(MaxPerfilUsuario perfil) async {
  78:     await MaxMemoryService.instance.definirPerfilAtivo(perfil.nome, infantil: perfil.infantil);
  79:     widget.onPerfilSelecionado?.call();
  80:     if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
  81:     MaxAssistantService.instance.falar('Olá, ${perfil.nome}! Perfil carregado. O que vamos assistir?');
  82:   }
